Opening makes the two copies and the dim on the UI thread (two 33 MB copies and one pass over 8.3 M pixels per 4K monitor, tens of ms, not measured). Committing a very long stroke repaints its covers once on release.
- **Open items:** (1) the showcase export (`Showcase/ShowcaseCapture`) renders the overlay window into a bitmap, which cannot see a native window or pop-ups, so its area-editor image will lack the picture and the bars; only for README images. (2) A brief flash of the window's background before the first paint was not ruled out. (3) The other screenshot windows (recording bar and frame, the OSD, the point picker) stay Avalonia windows; they are small or rarely redrawn. (4) `KeyLearner`, `WinKeyHook`, `MouseHook` still declare their own `GetModuleHandle`; `WindowNative` has one.
- **Unverified (nothing was opened):** the speed itself; that GDI drawing in a child of Avalonia's WinUI-composited window shows (Avalonia binds its composition target below child windows, `CreateDesktopWindowTarget(hwnd, isTopmost: false)`, and its `NativeControlHost` is made for child windows, but not seen here); the pop-ups' places, see-through corners and tooltips over the picture; the text box in a pop-up taking the keys; the strong Stay on top with pop-ups; mixed-DPI monitors; both themes; the colour fix in the real window.

## Screenshot overlay speed, second pass (17 Nov; its picture cache and canvas layers were replaced by the third pass above) - compiled only (HEAD plus these files in a throwaway copy under %TEMP%, 0 errors); `MarkRenderTests`, `ScreenshotTests`, `BlankScreenshotTests` run (62 passed, 2 new); nothing captured, drawn or shown on the PC

- **The main cause, found in Avalonia 11.3.6 and Skia (`Screenshot/GpuPictureCache.cs`):** Avalonia gives Skia a texture cache of about 28 MB (`SkiaOptions.MaxGpuResourceSizeBytes`). Skia uploads a picture bigger than half the cache in tiles on every frame it is drawn (new tile images each frame, so nothing is reused) and drops an upload that does not fit at the end of the frame. A 4K monitor's picture (33 MB) is over, so every frame re-sent the picture under the changed area to the graphics card, twice (the dimmed picture and the bright selection); the first pass made that area smaller, which is why it helped only a little. At 16K that is hundreds of MB per frame. Now `CaptureSession` works out the cache the session needs (`BytesFor`: more than twice the largest picture, at least all of them, plus Avalonia's own default) and each `PictureLayer` records a `GpuPictureCache.Raise` draw step before the picture, which raises Skia's limit on the render thread (`ISkiaSharpApiLeaseFeature`, `GRContext.SetResourceCacheLimit`). Each picture is then uploaded once and reused on every frame. Nothing happens when drawing is not on the graphics card (software rendering, the tests).
- **Once per frame (`Services/OncePerFrame.cs`):** the canvas's reaction to a selection change (new clip, frame) and the window's bar placement run at most once per drawn frame, just before it (`RequestAnimationFrame`, guarded); a 1000 Hz mouse sent about sixteen moves per frame, each doing that work. The view-model still takes every pointer move (the pen keeps every point).
- **Bars without layout (`CaptureOverlay.axaml.cs`):** the size label, the toolbar and the action bar are placed by a `TranslateTransform` instead of `Canvas.Left/Top`, which made a layout pass on every move. Pop-downs and tooltips follow render transforms. The text box still uses `Canvas.Left/Top` (set once when it opens).
- **Fewer binding updates (`CaptureOverlayViewModel.OnSelectionChanged`):** `HasSelection` and `ShowBars` are announced only when a selection appears or goes, `SizeText` only when the width or height changes; before, each move re-evaluated four reflection bindings. Moving a selection now updates no binding.
- **Pen and marker in progress (`Marks.cs`, `CaptureCanvas.cs`):** a `StrokeMark` being drawn is cut into settled parts of 64 steps and the growing rest (`Mark.DrawSettled` / `SettledCount` / `DrawGrowing` / `InProgressOpacity`, defaults keep the line, arrow and box as they were). Each move rebuilds and repaints only the rest; the settled layer is recorded again once per 64 points, and Skia can keep each settled part's outline. Before, every move rebuilt and repainted the whole line, so a long scribble got slower as it grew. The marker's see-through strength is applied to both layers together by an opacity mask on their group (Avalonia's `Opacity` fades each shape on its own, which would darken where parts meet). The finished line is still one path, so finished drawings and the export are exactly as before. `LongStroke_LooksTheSameWhileDrawnAsWhenFinished` checks pen and marker: only anti-aliasing differs (Skia picks another edge rasteriser for a short path, up to 24 of 255 on an edge pixel); without the group mask the marker differs by 31 where parts meet, with it 7.
- **Hard-coded on purpose:** the opacity mask's brush is `Colors.Black` at the marker's strength in `CaptureCanvas.ShowNewPreview`: a mask uses only its strength, the colour is never seen, so it is not a theme colour. The 64-step part length is a private constant on `StrokeMark` (a drawing detail, not a timing).
- **Checked and left as they are:** Avalonia 11.3's compositor (its `ServerCompositionVisual` source) counts a geometry clip's bounds in the area it repaints, so the first pass's clip change does repaint only around the old and new selection (that pass's open "unverified" point); the picture is drawn at its own pixel size (no scaling per frame; bilinear at 1:1 costs nothing, nearest would change the look at fractional scales); the dim is one static layer (four rectangles around the selection would repaint the whole monitor on every move); hit testing (every overlay layer is not hit-testable, the bars hide while dragging); the cursor (changed only when its shape differs); there is no magnifier. Changing the clip still makes a new `RectangleGeometry`, now once per frame and only when it differs.
- **Skipped, with reasons:** (1) Avalonia repaints only the changed area into its own full-window layer but then copies the whole layer to the screen every frame; that is inside Avalonia's compositor and the window's composition mode is app-wide, so it was not changed (it is graphics-card work, about one monitor's picture per frame). (2) The initial grab stays GDI `BitBlt` with `CAPTUREBLT` into a DIB and one copy into the bitmap: Desktop Duplication needs its own set-up per monitor (slower for one picture) and leaves layered windows out on some systems; dropping `CAPTUREBLT` is faster but can miss layered windows; writing straight into a `WriteableBitmap` would make the renderer re-upload it. (3) One `BitBlt` for the whole virtual desktop instead of one per monitor saves only call overhead and can need far more memory when monitors are not in a rectangle. (4) A picture wider or taller than the graphics card's largest texture (16384 pixels on Direct3D 11) is still tiled by Skia on every frame.
- **Open items:** (1) the raised Skia cache limit stays raised until OmniControl closes (it can only be changed on the render thread while a frame is drawn, and after the overlay closes none of its frames are drawn); Skia frees each picture's texture when the picture is released, but may keep it as a reusable scratch texture up to the raised limit, so after a 16K capture up to about one monitor's picture of graphics memory may stay in use until it is needed for something else. (2) `OverlayTopmost.PutOnTop` uses `SWP_SHOWWINDOW` every 250 ms in the strong mode; if that makes Windows send a paint, Avalonia would repaint the whole window then. Not confirmed; `SWP_NOREDRAW` could be tried if the strong mode stutters.
- **Unverified:** the overlay was not opened. Not checked: the speed itself (expected: no picture upload per frame after the first, so moving and resizing cost about the changed area once plus Avalonia's full-window copy), that the render thread's context is the one that draws the picture on every monitor, both themes, the bars' pop-downs and tooltips at their new transforms, mixed-DPI monitors, the marker's group mask on the graphics card, the showcase export.

## Recording on the graphics card (17 Nov) - compiled only (Core, the app and the test project in a throwaway folder, 0 errors); `VideoRecordingTests` and `ScreenshotTests` run (67 passed, 3 new); nothing captured, recorded or shown on the PC

- **MP4 frames stay on the graphics card (open item 1 of the speed audit):** with "Auto (graphics card)" on, `ScreenRecorder` first opens `Core/Engine/Screenshot/DesktopDuplication.cs` (DXGI Desktop Duplication of the monitor that holds the whole area, on the adapter that drives it; the area is copied on the card into a texture, the pointer drawn into a second, GDI-compatible texture through `IDXGISurface1::GetDC`) and `Mp4Writer.OpenOnGpu` (the D3D11 device given to the Sink Writer through a DXGI device manager, `MF_SINK_WRITER_D3D_MANAGER` + hardware transforms, input ARGB32 textures, so Media Foundation's video processor converts to NV12 on the card and the hardware encoder takes it). Joined by `FrameSources.cs` (`IFrameSource`: `GpuFrames` / `GdiFrames`). Desktop Duplication was chosen over Windows.Graphics.Capture because WGC draws a yellow border on the recorded monitor unless the app targets Windows 11 SDK contracts (the projects target 10.0.19041), and WGC needs WinRT interop on top; Desktop Duplication shows nothing, leaves the pointer out (it is drawn in as before) and honours "excluded from capture" like GDI.
- **Fallback, honest:** the GDI path (unchanged) is used when the hardware encoder is off, the area spans monitors, the monitor is turned, Direct3D 11 video support or the duplication is refused, or the Sink Writer cannot take textures from that device; one line in `errors-log.txt` says which. If Windows takes the duplication away mid-recording (display change, secure desktop) the area is copied with GDI into the same texture until it can be duplicated again (`CoreTiming.RecordDuplicationRetryMs`), said once in the log. When every pooled texture is still with the encoder a frame is skipped (the video stays in time, like any late frame).
- **Pooling (open item 2):** `SamplePool.cs` = Media Foundation's video sample allocator (`MFCreateVideoSampleAllocatorEx`): textures on the graphics-card path, system-memory buffers on the GDI path (rows written through `IMF2DBuffer::Lock2D` with its own pitch); a sample goes back to the pool when the Sink Writer and encoder release it, so nothing guesses their lifetime. At most 8 samples. On the GDI path a pool Windows refuses, or an empty one, means a fresh buffer for that frame, exactly as before.
- **Pointer:** one drawing for both paths, `PointerPainter.cs` (`PointerLook` moved there): the click circle is a premultiplied picture made once and blended with `AlphaBlend`, then `DrawIconEx`. The GDI path used to blend the circle in managed code; the colour maths is the same (rounding may differ by one level).
- **GIF (open item 3):** `QueuedVideoSink.cs` runs `GifWriter` on a worker ("GIF encoding") with a one-frame queue: while the worker is busy and a frame already waits, a new frame is dropped, so the waiting one lasts longer (no drift, no growing queue); a worker failure is thrown on the recording thread. `GifWriter` itself is unchanged (same bytes).
- **Expected gain (not measured):** 4K/60 MP4 no longer does a GDI copy (about 33 MB) plus a software colour conversion per frame on the CPU, nor a 33 MB buffer allocation per frame; the CPU cost per frame becomes a few small Direct3D calls and the pointer drawing. GIF: capture and palette/compression overlap, so fewer frames are skipped on large areas (up to about twice the frame rate where encoding was the bottleneck).
- **Open items:** a failure of the graphics-card writer after it opened (the encoder refusing a sample, device removed) ends the recording as failed rather than switching to GDI. HDR monitors: `DuplicateOutput` may be refused there (then GDI); `DuplicateOutput1` is not used. Whether GDI drawing on the GDI-compatible texture stays on the graphics card depends on the driver.
- **Unverified:** nothing was recorded. Not checked: that Desktop Duplication starts on this PC (and on hybrid laptops), that the Sink Writer inserts the graphics card's colour converter for ARGB32 textures, the video's colours and orientation, that the system-memory sample allocator works without a device manager (if not, the old per-frame buffer is used), that excluded windows stay out of the duplicated image, the pointer and click circle on the texture, the GIF worker on a real recording, the gain itself.

## Screenshot and recording speed audit (17 Nov) - compiled only (the only error in the throwaway build was the FX agent's unfinished `Fx/FxWindowService.cs`); nothing captured, drawn, recorded or shown on the PC

- **Overlay layers (`Screenshot/CaptureCanvas.cs`):** before, every pointer move during a selection drag recorded the full-monitor dim (four rectangles) and the finished drawings again, so the renderer repainted the whole monitor each move (4K x several screens). Now: picture and dim over the whole monitor are recorded once; the bright selection is a second draw of the same picture inside a `Panel` whose `Clip` is the selection, which also holds the finished drawings and the drawing in progress. A selection change only sets that clip and records the thin frame (cached pen, no array per render). New view-model event `MarksChanged` (draw, undo, redo, text) is the only thing that records the finished drawings again; `Redraw` now means "the selection moved". Looks unchanged: the inside of the selection is the same opaque picture over the dim.
- **One picture per monitor (`ImageBitmaps`, `ScreenCapture.Capture<T>`):** the overlay copied each screen into a managed array (plus a byte-by-byte alpha pass), then copied that into a `WriteableBitmap`, and kept both. Now `ImageBitmaps.Capture` builds an immutable Avalonia `Bitmap` straight from Windows' DIB (Core's `Capture<T>(area, use)` hands the pixels to a callback), so each 4K monitor holds about 33 MB instead of 66 MB and opens with one copy less. The QR reader and the blur read their part back with `ImageBitmaps.Crop` (`Bitmap.CopyPixels`); an immutable bitmap is used because `WriteableBitmap.CopyPixels` locks it, which makes the renderer upload the whole picture to the graphics card again. `ScreenImage.MakeOpaque` (vectorised) is the one alpha pass, used by capture, crop and export.
- **Pen and marker:** a pointer move to the same point no longer adds a point to the line.
- **GIF (`Core/Engine/Screenshot/GifWriter.cs`):** the LZW table was a `Dictionary` looked up on every pixel; now the classic open-addressed table (5003 slots, double hashing), cleared with one `Array.Fill`. Expected several times faster per frame; GIF output bytes are identical (same codes). Tests added: `Gif_NoisyFrameReadsBackExactly` (fills and resets the table), `MakeOpaque_SetsEveryAlphaAndKeepsTheColours`.
- **Checked and left as they are:** the recording thread already skips late frames rather than queuing, the Sink Writer throttles `WriteSample` (back-pressure), timers are 250 ms (on-top refresh, bar timer), the cursor is only changed when its shape differs, the drawing in progress rebuilds its geometry once per rendered frame (not per pointer event).
- **Open items:** (1) to (3) were done the same day, see "Recording on the graphics card (17 Nov)".
- **Unverified:** that Avalonia repaints only around the selection when the clip changes (its compositor decides the dirty area; at worst it is the full monitor as before), the overlay at 4K/5K and on mixed-DPI monitors, both themes, the blur and QR on the new crop path, GIF output in browsers.

## FX device folders (17 Nov) - built to `docs/mockups/fx-device-folders.html`; compiled into a throwaway folder (0 errors); not run, nothing clicked, no device switched, no Equalizer APO file touched

- **Model (kept from the earlier pass):** a folder is a flat `FxSlot` of kind `folder` (`Audio/FxFolders.cs`, `FxSlot.DeviceId`); the effects after it up to the next folder are its own, the ones before the first folder run for every device. `FxFolders.ChainFor(chain, deviceId)` is the one place that decides what runs on which device; `RoutePlanner`, `EffectsEngine.BuildChain` (rebuilt when the bus device changes) and `ApoChains` (the APO chain written for `MasterApoDevice`) use it, so a folder's effects run only on its device through whichever method that device has; chains saved before folders load and run as before. Device facts are read live by `Fx/FxDeviceSource` (never a stored list).
- **Now drawn (this pass):** `FxChainPanel.axaml` row template (folder row and effect row, `chainfolder` / `chainindent` / `chainhint` styles), the "Device folder" pill (`IconGlyph.pillglyph`, `PillGlyphSize`) between Add and Remove (master chain only, `FxChainViewModel.CanAddFolder`), `Fx/FxFolderEditor.axaml` (glyph, name, On switch; cards Device = `MenuChoiceItem` with "Playback devices" header and both method tags, Method = `FactItem` with the read-only tag and the pill "Change in Mixer", Mix = `SliderItem`; the folder's effects as `Button.menurow` rows; "Add effect here..."), a closed folder hides its effects (`FxSlotViewModel.Shown`, kept by `Renumber`), the toolbar's effect controls hide for a folder (`ShowsEffectTools`). The Add effect window's first entry is "Device folder" (only offered on the master chain) and its foot is the master `Border.footer`. A folder is removed, moved (Alt+Up/Down, drag) and shifted with its effects (`WithChildren`, `StepFolder`).
- **Differences from the mock-up:** none known. The folder effects list uses `Button.menurow` rows; the chevron is a `Button.step` with the shared chevron icons. "Save folder as preset..." keeps the folder under a first free name ("My folder") and opens the preset manager at "Device folders" (Volume), where it is renamed: the effect presets work the same way, there is no name prompt anywhere.
- **Save / Load chain (17 Nov, `docs/mockups/fx-save-load-chain.html`):** FX menu items after Move down; `Fx/FxChainFile.cs` writes `{Format:"omnifx", Chain:[FxSlot...]}` (indented json, temp file then move); load validates the format and every slot kind, refuses device folders on a non-master chain, replaces the chain like a folder preset does (`FxChainViewModel.LoadFrom`), one sentence in a `MessageWindow` on failure. Ctrl+S / Ctrl+O are routed by `FxWindow` like the other FX shortcuts (the FX menu items are not registered hotkeys, so these are not either). There is no undo in the FX window, so Load is not undoable. "Change in Mixer" now runs `VolumePageViewModel.ShowMixerFor(deviceId)`: the Mixer rows are the master, programs and combined devices, never a real playback device, so a folder lands on a combined speaker whose cable is that device, else on the master row (in practice mostly the master).
- **Added after the first pass (same day):** folder presets (`Audio/PresetContent.cs` `FolderPreset`: device, Mix, effects with all their values, kept in `presets.json` beside the effect presets by `UserPresetStore`; `Fx/Presets/FolderPresets.cs` is the preset group "Device folders", `volume.fx.folder`; applying one replaces the effects and Mix of the master's selected folder, else its first, else makes a folder for the preset's device; through the master's FX window when open (`FxChainViewModel.FolderPresets.cs`) else the saved chain). "Change in Mixer" is a `Button.link` (`FactItem.ActionIsLink`) and runs `VolumePageViewModel.ShowMixerMaster` (switches to Advanced, Mixer tab, opens Outputs, moves the keyboard to the master row, whose Method every playback device uses; kept as a pending reveal until the page is on screen). Mix: OmniControl's own engine mixes a folder (`MixOpenStage`/`MixCloseStage` around its effects, `ChainBuilder.BuildParts`, `FxFolders.PartsFor`); Equalizer APO has no dry/wet mix, so with Mix below 100 % none of the folder's effects run there (`FxFolders.ApoChainFor` gives them the folder's mix, the writer lists them as "mix below 100 %", the Method card says so). Remove on a folder that holds effects asks (`Confirm.Delete`, "Don't ask again" offered); a folder block dropped inside another folder or among the top-level effects goes to the nearest folder boundary (`FolderBoundary`); the Volume list's effect count skips folders (`FxSlot.EffectCount`); in the chain list Up/Down skip the rows of a closed folder and wrap, Left/Right close/open the selected folder.
- **Still open:** the FX window's total response graph does not include a folder's Mix (it sums the effects that run for the playing device as if the Mix were 100 %); the dry sound of a Mix is not delayed to match a plug-in's latency (same as an effect's own Wet). Equalizer APO blends a Mix too since 18 Nov (see "FX Mix in Equalizer APO" above).
- **Not verified:** everything on screen (row layout at the narrowest FX window, both themes, hover and focus, dimming, rubber band over hidden rows, the new link and the Save pill, the preset manager's new group, keyboard stepping and wrapping); the pop-down device menu; a folder added in a chain with an unplugged device; that the cable bus takes over a folder's effects the moment Windows' default changes; that the Mix blend sounds right (compiled only, nothing played); that Equalizer APO drops the folder as described; the jump to the Mixer's master row (scrolling and focus once the tab is laid out).


## Effects stay in Equalizer APO (17 Nov) - compiled in scratch folders (app and tests, 0 errors); `MasterMethodTests` run (15 passed); nothing launched, no Equalizer APO file read, written or edited on the PC
- **Cause:** Equalizer APO's Configuration Editor writes back the config.txt it loaded, without OmniControl's `Include: OmniControl.txt`; `ApoChains` only compared with its own memory, so it never noticed.
- **Now:** `Audio/Engine/ApoWatch` (FileSystemWatcher per config folder + watchdog every `Timing.ApoWatchdog`, reads off the UI thread, guarded, disposed first at exit) -> `EffectsEngine.OnApoFound` -> `ApoKeeper.Take` (UI thread): with "Keep OmniControl's effects in place" on (default, `MixerSettings.KeepFxInPlace`, `MixerService.SetKeepFxInPlace`) the line and file are put back at once (`ApoChains.PutBack`), at most once per `Timing.ApoRestoreGap`, a loop (`ApoRestoreLoopCount` in `ApoRestoreLoopWindow`) said once through `Report`; then the chain file is touched once `Timing.ApoReloadSettle` after the last write (also after every EQ drag burst: the missed-reload fix). Off: the folder is displaced, its rows say `EffectsRules.ApoDisplaced` (`StatusKind.ApoDisplaced`, a program on an APO cable stops as a failed run until it is back), the FX window's status strip shows a `Button.link` "Put back". Every reconcile (device, default and support changes) asks for a look; a watchdog look more than `Timing.ApoResumeGap` late = woke from sleep, so `EffectsSupportService.Refresh` runs too. Effects off: the target is "clean", so the keeper only ever removes. The switch: FX window Options (a checkbox menu item like Keep on top, the menu's own pattern) and App settings > Plug-ins (`ToggleItem`), state `volume.fx.keepinplace` (Volume page).
- **Also fixed:** include line moved to the very top (`ApoConfigFiles.Placed`); failed writes retried after `Timing.ApoRetryGap` instead of on every reconcile; IR Auto level measured off the UI thread (`IrLevels`, a section waits); IR file version (`Ir/IrFileStamp`) in the APO text and in `ApoWatchInput.IrFiles`, a replacement reloads both engines (`EffectsEngine.ReloadIrFiles`); `IrStage.Load` ends in a kernel for any failure (logged), and a missing IR file is listed as not run (`ChainBuilder.IrStageOf`). Own effects in config.txt: one neutral `TagView` in the FX window's strip.
- **Not verified:** that Equalizer APO reloads on a write-time-only change (`ApoConfigFiles.Touch`, chosen because its folder watch reports last-write changes); resume detection by the watchdog's lateness rather than a Windows power event (no power-event hook exists in Core yet; open item); behaviour against the real Configuration Editor. **Open item:** `AppSettingsPageViewModel` now takes 11 constructor parameters (it gets `MixerService` for the switch); split a concern out when it is next touched.

## Tailscale on an already paired PC (17 Nov), built to `docs/mockups/other-pcs-tailscale-connect.html`; compiled only (the only errors were other agents' unfinished Screenshot files); nothing run
- **Cause of the bug.** `SetRoute(Tailscale)` stored the route at once, so the row said "via Tailscale" (the tag followed the chosen route) and the old address box was shown; the pairing error came from typing that address into Pair a PC.
- **Now.** `PairedPc.TailscaleAddress` (own field; `Address` is only the typed "Other address"; old saves are migrated at load). `PcLink.ViaTailscale` = the other end's address is in Tailscale's ranges, read from the socket, so tags say the real route. `SetRoute(Tailscale)` keeps the current link and starts `CheckPairTailscale(id)` (`OtherPcsService.TailscalePair.cs`): `TailscaleChecks.Build` with `peerName` adds the "<PC> on your Tailscale" row (peer found by saved address, else by name, case-insensitive) and "Reaches OmniControl on it". All ticked = `TailscaleWorks`: keep the peer's address, send ours in a `ts-address` message (`PcMsgType.TailscaleAddress`, only Tailscale-range addresses accepted) over the current link, close the link; both redial over Tailscale. The receiver sets itself to Tailscale too (as pairing over the internet does). `RecheckTailscalePairs` re-checks every `Timing.PcTailscaleRecheck` while a PC is set to Tailscale and not carried by it, so the stage comes back if Tailscale stops. A PC set to Tailscale whose Tailscale dial failed is found on the network meanwhile (`_tailscaleFailed`, cleared when a Tailscale link attaches or the checks pass again).
- **One checklist piece.** `Pages/OtherPcs/TailscaleChecklist` (+ `TailscaleCheckItem`, `TailscaleChecks`) is used by Pair a PC and by `PcDetail`'s stage; `RunTailscaleFix(checkId, recheck)` takes what to re-read.
- **Deviations / open.** The checklist keeps Pair a PC's separate "Running" and "Signed in" rows (the mock-up abbreviates them as one). "How to add it" opens Tailscale's install page. The switch dials Tailscale BESIDE the working LAN link; `Attach` replaces the LAN link only once the Tailscale link is open and authenticated. On failure LAN stays, the stage shows the failed step (`PairSwitchProblem`) and no new try is made for `Timing.PcTailscaleSwitchBackoff`. Unverified: everything at run time (two PCs, real Tailscale), wording at the narrowest window and in both themes.

## Other PCs over the internet via Tailscale (17 Nov), built to `docs/mockups/other-pcs-tailscale.html`; compiled only (throwaway Release build, 0 errors); nothing run, no Tailscale or firewall touched, no test written
- **Layers.** Core `Engine/Tailscale/`: `TailscaleCli` (finds tailscale.exe, runs it hidden, Start / SignIn fixes), `TailscaleStatus.cs` (`TailscaleStatusReader.Read` = `tailscale status --json`, `Parse`, `IsTailscaleAddress`), `TailscaleFirewall` (rule "OmniControl on Tailscale": exists = netsh exit code; Allow = `HelperProcess.RunElevated` of netsh, scoped to the tailnet ranges, any profile); `Engine/TcpReach.cs`; waits in `CoreTiming.Tailscale*`/`TcpReachWaitMs`. App `OtherPcs/TailscaleChecks.cs` (THE checklist: order, words, sentence, fix; pure) and `OtherPcsService.Tailscale.cs` (reads off the UI thread, fixes, background re-check of offline Tailscale-paired PCs every `Timing.PcTailscaleRecheck`). Page: `OtherPcsPageViewModel.Internet.cs`, `TailscaleCheckItem`, the stage in `OtherPcsPage.axaml`; `Styles/Tables.axaml` got `IconGlyph.cautionmark` and `Ellipse.pendingdot` (checklist marks).
- **Two-way pairing.** `PairMsg.Addr`: the PC pairing over the internet sends its Tailscale address; the other answers with its own (only if it has one) and both addresses are part of the proved transcript. Only addresses in Tailscale's ranges are kept. `PcRoute.Tailscale` (new value) is stored with that address; either PC dials it (the existing dedupe of two links applies). A PC that has Tailscale but pairs on the LAN stores nothing.
- **Deviations from the mock-up.** The checklist's Connected line has no fix (a Tailscale that is switched off fails Running, whose fix is "Start Tailscale" = `tailscale up`); the mock-up's "Code [..] Pair" is the existing two steps (Pair asks the other PC, which shows the code; the code is then typed in the existing code row); an offline Tailscale peer is not listed in the pick menu; the Connect chip on a PC now offers LAN, Tailscale and Other address (the old "Tailscale / ZeroTier" is "Other address"), and the old "online" tag no longer says Tailscale for an address route.
- **Unverified.** The exact JSON of `tailscale status --json` was written from memory of its documented fields (BackendState, Version, Self, Peer, User, TailscaleIPs, OS, Online); `tailscale up` / `login` fixes; the netsh rule syntax and that Windows Firewall lets the tailnet in with it; the pairing with a real second PC; the wording on the narrowest window and in both themes (not viewed).

## PC nickname and route probe (17 Nov), built to `docs/mockups/pc-nickname.html`; compiled only (throwaway Release build); nothing run or tried on a network
- **Nickname.** `PairedPc.Nickname` (saved with the pair, never sent) and `DisplayName`; `OtherPcsService.Rename` saves and re-registers the PC's states and actions under the new group name (`Reregister`, values kept). Right-click in the PC list opens the shared `ListMenuFlyout` + `MenuRowList` with "Give nickname..."; the question is `DialogService.AskTextAsync` (`DialogMessage.Input`, a field in `MessageWindow`, Enter = OK). Differences from the mock-up: the menu row has no pencil glyph (`MenuRowItem` has no icon field, the Simulation step menu has none either); the dialog's confirm is the shared `usebtn` OK, not `Button.primary`.
- **Route probe** (`OtherPcsService.Probe.cs`, `PcProtocol.WantsProbe`, `PcMsgType.Pong`, `Timing.PcProbe*`): a short connection to the route's address (LAN beacon address, typed address or Tailscale address) runs the session proof, sends a sealed ping with a fresh nonce and needs the pong that repeats it; round trip measured. `RouteConnected` = a success over the CURRENT route within `PcProbeFresh`; the list tag and note read it (`PcItem.Refresh`), the Tailscale checklist's last row is the same probe (its time as the row's tag, its reason as the sentence), and the switch to Tailscale starts only after that probe passes. A failed Tailscale probe puts the PC back to LAN dialling. `Attach` refuses a Tailscale link while this PC's route for that PC is not Tailscale (this was why the list kept saying "via Tailscale" after the chip was set to LAN: the other PC kept dialling over Tailscale).
- **Why the switch did not happen (not proven, no logs, nothing run):** a failed dial to an old stored Tailscale address set a two-minute back-off that also blocked the try with the fresh address, and the plain TCP "reaches" row passed without any authentication. Both are fixed. Unverified: that this is the owner's case. A LAN PC with no recent beacon is shown "not seen on this network" even if a link exists from its side.
- Open: unit tests for the probe and `Rename` were not written.

## Other PCs (17 Nov) - basic only, built to `docs/mockups/other-pcs.html` (LAN method only; the relay is not built); compiled only (throwaway Release build into a temp folder, 0 errors); no test written or run; nothing listened, broadcast, paired, connected, sent, played or switched on the PC or the network

- **Decision.** Basic only: anyone who wants more uses Windows Remote Desktop. The advanced per-page sections, the share window, the Share and with chips, `ShareChoice` / `ShareMode`, per-item share choices and storage, `HotkeyAction.Risky` and the old `Common` bool are gone.
- **Files.** `App/OtherPcs/*` (service and protocol), `App/Pages/OtherPcs/*` (page: `OtherPcsPage`, `PcDetail`, `PcItem`, `PcMirror`), `States/CommonGroups.cs`, `DataStores.OtherPcs` (`other-pcs.json`: pairs, `LastSeen`, last `Catalog`), `Timing.Pc*`, `Guard.RunAsync`, `LaunchLimiter`. Owners of the basic set: Displays (screen power, brightness, "Turn off displays"), Volume (master volume, mute, default speaker, mic mute, and the media entries in `VolumePageViewModel.Media.cs` over `Core/Engine/Media/NowPlaying`), Devices (a power switch per device), Automation (`PowerStates` battery only when the PC has one, `PowerActions` Lock, Sign out, Sleep, Restart, Shut down, `KeepAwakeEntries` over `Core/Engine/KeepAwake`).
- **What is shared.** Exactly what owners flag `Common = CommonGroups.X` on their master entry (state or action), with every paired PC, from pairing on; nothing to choose. `HotkeyAction.ConfirmRun` (Restart, Shut down) makes the other PC's page ask first through `DialogService`. Both the catalog sent and every request are checked on the sharing side against `Common` (the id is looked up in this PC's own master list, never trusted from the message).
- **Registration (kept).** Each paired PC's basic set is registered in `HotkeyRegistry` / `StateRegistry` as `pc:<pc id>:<its id>` (stable per PC id, so keys survive a rename), plainly named ("Lock", "Mute"), category and picker section "Other PCs", group / heading = the PC's name; so the Actions and hotkeys window, the mouse, gesture and rule pickers, search and automation triggers have them. They are used by the page's cards and chips and by hotkeys and rules. The last catalog is stored with the pair, so the entries are listed after a restart while the PC is offline: actions carry `UnavailableReason` "<PC> is not connected.", states a `Liveness.NotSure` sentence. Unpairing removes them. The rest of the protocol (pairing by numeric comparison, AES-GCM link, rate limits, `RunChain`) is unchanged.
- **Differences from the mock-up.** (1) The Now playing pills carry their words with their icons ("Previous track", "Play or pause media", "Next track"), not a glyph alone, and the card's icon is the library's music icon, not the playing app's own picture (only text travels between PCs). (2) A group made only of switches (Smart devices) gets the shared master switch and "N of M on" in its header, as every such group does. (3) The battery is the Power header's muted text; Keep awake is full width because a lone switch is full width. (4) The pill order is the registration order of the master entries, not a list on the page. (5) Restart and Shut down confirm in the standard dialog without "Don't ask again". (6) Two PCs with the same name share one heading in the pickers (the heading is the name); they are not told apart there.
- **Shared pieces added for the mock-up (17 Nov).** `SliderItem.Lead` (a `ChoiceItem` drawn as a `Controls/ChoiceChip` at the left of the slider, inside the card; any page may set it; `PcMirror` makes Sound's output the lead of its volume slider), `Controls/ChoiceChip` (a `ChoiceItem` as the shared `ChipMenu` with a ticked list), `FactItem.Stacked` + `Pills` (a media line: icon, value over muted note, pills at the right) over `Controls/ChipItem` (a code-made `ChoiceChips` pill, `IsSelected` = accent), `Services/NowPlayingText` (the one format of the Now playing state: "Title ‖ Artist · App", number 1 playing / 0 paused). Picker ordering: `PickerModel.InGroups` lists a section's group headings by name (ignoring case) after the entries without one, so the groups of Other PCs come PC by PC in name order; this rule is for every section, so another section's groups that were in registration order are now in name order too.
- **Unverified.** Every control on the other PC; the new group order in the Actions window and the other pickers (not looked at); the Sound chip and the Now playing card at the narrowest window; how the chosen icon ids look; Windows' media session with browsers; Keep awake letting go on exit; all bindings at run time (compiled bindings are off); two PCs talking.
- **Open items.** The relay method. Names of other PCs are fixed at pairing. No unit test exists for the crypto, `NowPlaying.AppName` or `PcMirror`.

## Import devices from another PC (17 Nov) - built to `docs/mockups/import-devices.html`; compiled only (the app builds with no error of its own; the only error in the throwaway build was another agent's `Fx/FxDeviceSource.cs` `VirtualCables`); nothing launched, paired, connected, sent or imported

- **Wire.** New messages `devices` (request, `Scripts` flag) and `devices-answer` (`Ok`, `Payload` = opaque JSON text, `Why`) over the existing paired, encrypted link (`OtherPcs/OtherPcsService.Devices.cs`). The Other PCs part only carries the text; `DeviceService.ShareSetups` (given to its constructor) makes it, `DeviceShareFormat` writes and reads it. The sharing side answers any paired PC without asking the owner (a paired PC can already run actions here), at most `Timing.PcDevicesBurstMax` per `PcDevicesBurstWindow`, and not above one frame. The last list asked without scripts is kept in `PairedPc.DeviceSetups` so an offline PC's devices can be imported.
- **Never sent:** passwords (they are only in the Credential Manager, never in a record), the account email (`MethodRecord.Email` emptied), the user's own icon picture (`IconImage`, `user:` icons), the text of recorded "type text" steps (could be a password), per-device auto-check overrides. **Scripts** = the user's own command templates (`ScriptProfile`s that are not built in); without "Include scripts" a method on one falls back to Auto-detect. The Scripts method itself (address, built-in template, set-up flags) is always copied.
- **Receiving is untrusted input:** `DeviceShareFormat.Read` checks the shape, caps counts and lengths, strips control characters, drops icons that are not in the library, and refuses an imported template whose command does not start with `kasa` (a template runs its first word as a program when it is not `kasa`, so an unchecked one would start any program on this PC). Imported ids are new; the source device id is kept as `DeviceRecord.OriginId`, and a device is "already here" when its id or origin matches a local id or origin. A template equal to an existing one is reused.
- **Deviations from the mock-up.** (1) The device icon is the device's own library icon, or the plug; the template's icon is not looked up (one more dependency for a muted detail). (2) "Include scripts" is a plain switch with the label and a "runs commands" caution tag at the group's foot beside the Import pill. (3) The group sits under the device pane and the empty row; "Import…" is a pill beside "+ Add device" and the empty row got an optional second link (`EmptyListRow.SecondLinkText` / `SecondCommand`), so the group's open state is its normal remembered one but "Import…" / the link also opens it and scrolls to it. (4) Rows are `ListBox.pick.compact` like the option list (click, or Space / Enter on the lit row, ticks); an "already here" row is disabled (dimmed) with a neutral tag. (5) An online PC that cannot answer shows its last known devices with the reason as the note.
- **Kept as imported, on purpose (mock-up: "not installed" status):** `Verified`, `SimVerified`, `SimAppReady` and the teaching come across as they were, so a simulation method on a PC without the emulator or the app shows the normal not-installed or failure line instead of looking unfinished.
- **Unverified.** The request and answer between two PCs; the group's look, the tick rows and the dimmed rows at the narrowest window and in both themes; keyboard use of the rows; the scroll to the group; the sentence after an import; Import with "Include scripts" on an offline PC (it says scripts need the PC online). No unit test for `DeviceShareFormat`.
- **Open.** The group refreshes when a PC's online state changes; a PC that gained devices while this page stays open needs the group to be reopened (Import…) or the page shown again.

## Taskbar button (17 Nov)
Cause (from code, not reproduced): `ShowInTaskbar` defaults to true, so the Actions and hotkeys window (set true in code), FX windows, About, preset and app-list managers each got a taskbar entry grouped with the main window, which can be hidden in the tray, so the group click could activate a window that was not shown. Now the base `Window` style in `Styles/Theme.axaml` sets False and only `MainWindow` sets True; the per-window False copies were removed. Compiled in a scratch folder only; not tried on screen.

## Grouped actions (17 Nov) - built to `docs/mockups/grouped-actions.html` and `docs/action-grouping-proposal.md` (without its 3 optional merges: rotate, layers, power modes); compiled in a scratch copy and `ActionPickerTests`, TrayTests, ShortcutGrouping run (46 passed); nothing launched, clicked or pressed on the PC

- A family is a field on the master entry: `HotkeyAction.Family` (`ActionFamily` id and title) and `FamilyRole`; a level action is the family of its `Level` (Up/Down) with nothing set. Set explicitly on the pointer numbers without a level, the pointer scheme (Next/Previous) and a smart device's On/Off/Toggle (per device and per method). Ids, keys, stored bindings, pages and app-wide search are unchanged; only the picker lists group (`PickerModel.Families.cs`, `PickChoice.Family` / `FamilyRole`).
- Differences from the mock-up: the key summary uses the words (Up, Down, Next) in place of arrows; the pick's direction chooser is a normal "Direction" setting row (chevrons, Left/Right, wheel) rather than a separate control; a row is greyed only when every direction is.
- Shared settings (how far a level moves) are written to every direction of the family; the device choice is shown once (first block) and copied to the others when it is changed (never silently on opening). If saved values already differ the pane shows the first direction's until edited.
- Open: a row's icon and "Show on its page" belong to the first direction; the tray's panel marks "In panel" on a row when any direction is in it.

## Don't ask again on confirmations (17 Nov) - compiled into a throwaway folder (0 errors); `ConfirmationMemoryTests` + `DataStoreBackupTests` run (12 passed); nothing launched or clicked

- A `DialogMessage` with a `RememberKey` and `RememberName` and a Confirm button but no Other answer shows "Don't ask again" (`MessageWindow`); `DialogService.ChooseAsync` returns Confirm without a window when it is remembered, and stores it only after Confirm with the box ticked (Cancel never). Saved in `confirmations.json` (backed up, wiped, in `DataStores`).

Deliberately NOT covered: allow-this-program/command approvals, backup import and wipe, installs and removals of the effects engine, restore defaults, keep-the-swap and anything with details or a second answer. "Clear all shortcuts?" and "Merge devices?" are simple confirmations still without a key (the first sits in `Picker/`, off limits for this job; the second was left out): give them `RememberKey` if wanted.

Unverified: the group's look, its keyboard walk (only the two lists use `RowEditing`; the header is not in a `GroupedCards` host) and the search results on screen.

## Action window ghost rows (17 Nov) - cause found from code only, not reproduced; compiled and `SearchPipelineTests` run; nothing launched or clicked
- Likely cause: `ListDiff` replaced a changed row in place (`target[i] = ...`), so a virtualised list kept the row's container and its old measured size and drew the new layout over the old (one-line over wrapped "no key"); the settings list was virtualised and cleared and refilled, so recycled rows of the previous action stayed under the new ones. Fix: `ListDiff` removes and inserts instead of replacing (all users: picker, search results, presets, tray, add-effect); the settings `ListBox` uses a plain `StackPanel`.
- Unverified: whether the ghost is gone in the app. If it still shows, the entries list's `VirtualizingStackPanel` (variable-height rows) is the next suspect.

## Icon picker search speed (17 Nov) - compiled in a scratch folder (0 errors); not run, nothing opened or typed on the PC
Slow before: every key rebuilt all sections and 523 cells (a new `IconCell` and resource lookup each) and re-ran `TextMatch.Any`, which normalises every icon's text again, all on the UI thread, in a non-virtualised grid. Now: `IconLibrary.Search` reads a haystack per icon built once; `IconPickerModel` runs it through `SearchRunner` (first letter at once, `Timing.SearchDebounce`, cancelled when superseded); sections and cells are kept and changed with `ListDiff` (library cells cached per part); `Timing.IconPickerFirstCells` cells show at once and the rest in the next frame. The grid is still not virtualised (open item if 523 cells ever feel heavy on first open). Unverified: real feel, and that the selection stays put while the rest fills in.

## Icon library, live plug state, drag to reorder, tray item menu (16 Nov) - built to `docs/mockups/icon-library.html`; Release built, launched, stayed up 6 s; `IconLibraryTests` + `TrayTests` run (35 passed); nothing clicked, dragged, picked or read on the PC

- **Icon library** (`App/Icons/`, see ARCHITECTURE "Icon library and picker"): 523 Tabler Icons 3.19.0 outline icons (grown from 103: each has a category and synonym keywords; the picker shows "All icons" as one headed part per category; "Suggested for this" ranks icons found by the words of the entry name, name matches first) (MIT; `third_party/tabler-icons/LICENSE`, NOTICE.txt so About lists it), fetched once from the npm package and converted into `Styles/Icons.axaml` (path data, drawn as 2 px round strokes on the 24 px grid by `Controls/IconGlyph`). Ids, names and keywords in `IconLibrary.All`. Values saved before keep working: the old glyph keys (`MicIcon`, `FxIcon`, `Device*Icon`...) and device icon ids read as library ids (`IconLibrary.Current`); the old glyph resources and the device icon list are gone, `DeviceIcons` keeps only the colours. A tray element's `Icon` saved as a section name still shows that section's icon.
- **User icons**: SVG (shapes only: path, circle, ellipse, rect, line, polyline, polygon; no transforms, text or gradients) or PNG (made at most 64 px), kept INSIDE `icons.json` (`DataStores.Icons`, Saved, not mergeable) so a backup carries them; registered as application resources under `user:<id>`. A picture picked for a device before the library (`device-icons` folder) still shows; a new pick replaces it.
- **Suggestions**: `HotkeyAction.SuggestedIcons` / `StateEntry.SuggestedIcons`, set on the master volume, mute, FX, mic mute, each display level, warm light, Open and Quit OmniControl. Default icon = first suggestion, else the entry's `Icon` (so brightness rows now show a sun, FX the sliders, bulbs an outline bulb). The picker's "Suggested for this" falls back to the own icon, then to icons found by the name's words.
- **Picker** (`Icons/IconPickerWindow`, one shared window through `IconPicker`): used by the tray's "Change icon…", a device's icon pop-down ("Change icon…" beside the colour dots; the old in-pop-down grid and "Own image…" moved into the picker) and the Actions and hotkeys window's settings pane ("Change icon…" after Show on its page; stored per action id in `icons.json`, applied to `HotkeyAction.ChosenIcon` as actions register).
- **Live plug state** (`Tray/TrayFlyoutModel.Rechecks.cs`): `StateEntry.Recheck` (`StateRecheck`: can it read now, and the read) is given by each device's power state: Scripts methods only (`DeviceService.CheckStateAsync(scriptsOnly: true)`, never simulation). On opening (after the first frame, `DrawFirst`) one device at a time, `Timing.TrayRecheckStagger` apart, never within 10 s of the last ask (`Timing.TrayRecheckMinGap`, pure `RecheckThrottle`); the old every-15-s timer is gone. Which devices read on opening: the Auto-check menu's new switch "Also when the tray panel opens" (`AutoChecks.OnTray`, on by default, follows "Same for every device"; `DeviceSettings.AutoCheckOnTray`, `DeviceRecord.AutoCheckOnTray`) or the choice "When the page or tray panel opens". **Tile marks (docs/mockups/tray-tile-states.html):** a tile always shows the state's real/last-read value and never lights from a click; `TrayTileItem.Mark` comes from `StateEntry.Liveness` (`StateLiveness`: acting, reading, failure, not-sure reason; the device's is `DeviceService.LivenessOf`, `Timing.DeviceStateStale` = 30 min, simulation-only is always not sure), re-read on `StateRegistry.LivenessChanged`. Marks are `Panel.tilemark` in `Styles/Tray.axaml` (sizes `TrayMark*`); the clock and warning are library icons (`alert` added, library now 103). Unverified: the spinner's `RotateTransform.Angle` animation and the mark's look were not seen running (no PC testing).
- **Drag to reorder**: `Controls/PanelDragReorder` (tiles grid and slider list; press, move 4 DIP; render transforms, others slide with `Timing.DragSlide` 110 ms cubic ease-out; Esc cancels before it hides the panel; no drag from a slider, the speaker chip or a remove cross; off while "Lock layout" is ticked). Maths in `Services/Reorder.TargetSlot` / `ShownSlot`; the stored list in pure `Tray/TrayOrder`: an item of a pattern (`devices.*.power`) is spelt out into one element per device in place, and the pattern stays after them for devices added later (`TrayTargets` leaves out of a pattern the ids the list names alone). Removing one device of a pattern takes the pattern out too (before: Remove took every device away).
- **Item menu** (tiles and sliders): Remove from panel, Change action… (the same picker as "+ Add" / "+ slider", In-panel marks, opened at the item's entry; the pick replaces it in place and keeps a user-set icon), Change icon…, Lock layout (tick, panel-wide, `TraySettings.LockLayout`), Show in OmniControl (`ShellViewModel.ShowInApp`: an action opens Actions and hotkeys at it; a state opens its page, which `PageViewModel.Reveal`s it: the Smart Devices page selects the device). Move left / right are gone. The menu is filled on `Opening`, so it is never shown empty (the likely "stray empty box"); the broken "Change icon…" (it re-opened a second menu after the first had closed) is now the picker.

**Differences from the spec.** "Use" is `Button.primary` (accent, as the mock-up asks) rather than `Button.usebtn`. "Own image…" adds the file to Your icons and uses it at once; "+ Add your own…" adds it and keeps the picker open (the mock-up does not say how they differ). The picker fills all its cells at once (100 small glyphs, no `DrawFirst` step needed). The icon cell and glyph are drawn by a custom `IconGlyph` control (strokes), not Path resources in `PathIcon`, because Tabler icons are line drawings that a filled `PathIcon` would draw as blobs. Drag starts after a 4 DIP move with the button held (no hold timer). A user icon is renamed in a Name box under the grid (shown when one of yours is selected), with Remove beside it. Grid cells are not keyboard-focusable; the arrows move the selection from the search box (mouse-first, AGENTS rule 5).

**Hard-coded on purpose.** `IconLibrary.All` (the 523 ids, names, categories, keywords) and `Styles/Icons.axaml` are data (the master list). The device "Change icon…" suggestions (bulb, plug, lamp) are a constant in `DeviceItem.Icon.cs`.

**Not verified (nothing was clicked or shown).** The look of every icon at 16-20 px in both themes; that Avalonia's `Flyout.Opening` fires before the menu draws; `PickerModel.ShowEntry` before the window opens really lands on the item; the drag's feel and that a tile press without moving still runs the tile (the drag captures the pointer only after the move); that a tile's capture handed to the list does not cancel the drag (guarded by the event's source); scripted device reads every 15 s with the panel open (they write the device log each time); SVG import of real files; PNG scaling.

## Tray flyout, double click and menu (16 Nov) - built to `docs/mockups/tray-flyout.html`; compiled only (throwaway builds of the app and the test project, 0 errors, no new warnings); `TrayTests` written, not run; nothing launched, clicked or shown on the PC

**What was built.** The existing tray icon (`Services/TrayService.cs`, moved to `Tray/TrayService.cs`, still the only one, made by `WindowLifecycle`) now: single click = the flyout, double click = Open OmniControl, right click = the menu.
- Clicks: `Tray/TrayClicks.cs` never delays the first click (the flyout opens at once); a second click within Windows' double-click time (`SystemCommands.DoubleClick`, read at each click; the one place it is read) closes the flyout and opens OmniControl. The flyout window is built once, hidden, after start-up (not in `StartupMode.Inert`); its lists are rebuilt only when a master list or edit mode changed (`_stale`), else only the values are taken again from the states' last readings (no device read happens on opening, so DDC is never read on the UI thread by the panel). Timing: `timing-log.txt` in the data folder (`Services/UiTiming`, shared with pages, windows and searches; `tray-log.txt` is no longer written). A click within `Timing.TrayReopenGrace` (400 ms) of the flyout hiding itself only closed it (the press on the icon takes its focus), so the icon toggles it.
- Flyout: `Tray/TrayFlyoutWindow` (borderless, topmost, see-through, `Window.trayflyout`), placed by the pure `TrayPlacement` at the click on that click's screen (`Screens.ScreenFromPoint`, its working area and scaling), again on `SizeChanged` / `ScalingChanged` (a move to a monitor of another DPI resizes it). Hides on `Deactivated` (click-away), Esc and Alt+F4; Tab / Shift+Tab go round it (`TabNavigation` Cycle) and the first control gets the keyboard on open. It stays open while a picker is up over it.
- Model: `Tray/TrayFlyoutModel(.Edit).cs`, made by `Shell/ShellViewModel.Tray.cs` (always made; in an export only the tray icon is not). Sliders = level states (`TraySliderItem`: moving it calls `StateEntry.Set`; a change from anywhere moves it through `StateRegistry.ValueChanged`); a slider may carry a choice state as a pick list (`ChipMenu` + `MenuRowList`: the speaker). Tiles (`TrayTileItem`): an action id runs through `HotkeyRegistry.Run` (the one run path: block rules apply), or an on/off state alone is set to the other value; lit (`Button.tile.on`) while its state is on. The lists are made again when the flyout opens and whenever a master list changes while it is open.
- Elements: `TrayElement` = an action id and/or a state id, a slider's choice state, and the section whose icon it shows (null: its own section's, from `HotkeyRegistry.SectionOf`; the icon choices are `HotkeyRegistry.Sections`, the live page list plus the extra sections, so there is no second icon list). An id may hold one `*` (`IdPattern`): `displays.*.brightness` = one row per display, `devices.*.power` = one tile per smart device. `TrayTargets` resolves them against the live lists; an id that matches nothing (or a state of the wrong kind) is simply not shown, and in edit mode its row says "Not available now".
- Settings: `TraySettings` (`tray.json`, `DataStores.Tray`, Saved: backed up and wiped). Null lists = the defaults (`TrayDefaults`, ids only); the first edit copies them in.
- Edit (the foot's Edit / Done): two `ListBox.rows.compact` lists with grip (`ListDragReorder`; the order through the new shared `Services/Reorder.Moved`, now also used by the device and method lists), icon, name and remove cross; a tile row's right-click menu (`ListMenuFlyout` + `MenuRowList`) picks its icon or removes it; "+ slider", "+ tile" and the "+ Add" tile open the Choose-the-action window (`TrayPicks`: every level state for a slider, a read-only one greyed; every master action and every on/off state for a tile; a state sits in its page's section). Every change is saved at once.
- Menu: `TrayDefaults.Menu`, four groups of action ids with a separator between: `app.show`, `app.actions.open` | `screenshot.area`, `screenshot.record` | `automation.pause` (ticked by `automation.paused`), `volume.fx` (ticked by `volume.master.fx`) | `app.settings.open`, `app.quit`. The words are the actions' titles. Rebuilt when a master list or a ticking state changes (`TrayFlyoutModel.MenuChanged`). The old Exit / Pause items, `TrayService.ShowPaused` and `ShellViewModel.RulesPaused / RulesChanged / ToggleRulesPause` are gone.
- New master actions: `app.quit` (App settings page), `app.settings.open` and `app.actions.open` (shell, filed under App settings like the Actions and hotkeys restore). New states (each with `Set`): `volume.master.fx`, `volume.mic.muted`, `displays.warmlight`, `automation.paused`. `Set` and `Short` added to `volume.master.level` ("Volume"), `volume.master.muted`, `volume.default.speaker` (makes that device the master's, as Plays on does), each display level (short name = the display's name) and each device's power (sends On / Off).
- Shared pieces added: `StateEntry.Set` / `Short` / `ShortName`, `HotkeyRegistry.SectionOf` / `Sections`, `Services/Reorder`, `Styles/Tray.axaml` (`Window.trayflyout`, `Border.flyoutpanel`, `Button.tile` + `.on`, `Grid.trayslider`, `Grid.trayedit`; sizes `Tray*` in App.axaml).

**Differences from the mock-up.**
- Right-click menu: Avalonia's own tray menu (`NativeMenu`, drawn by the Fluent theme), not the `listmenu` style. Avalonia's `TrayIcon` gives no right-click event of its own, so the `listmenu` look would need OmniControl's own notification icon in Core (the brief said to extend the existing tray). Owner to decide.
- "Effects off" is the master action "Toggle master FX" with a tick (there is no "all effects off" action); the menu's words are the actions' titles ("Screenshot: area (edit)", "Quit OmniControl").
- Icons are the sections' icons (the Displays page's for brightness, the Volume page's for volume, FX and mic, the Smart devices page's for devices), not a sun and a speaker; a tile's "change icon" offers the section icons.
- The laptop row is called "Built-in display" (the page's own name), trimmed in the 96 px name column; the mock-up says "Laptop".
- The foot's Edit is a link without a pencil glyph; it reads "Done" in edit mode.
- Edit mode shows the tiles as a row list (drag the grip) rather than dragging the tiles in their grid; the right-click is on the tile's row.
- Rotate is "Rotate to Portrait" (`displays.rotate.portrait`): there is no "rotate to the next" action.
- A slider added in edit mode cannot be given a pick list (only the default volume row has one).

**Hard-coded on purpose.** `TrayDefaults` types the default ids as a saved tray.json would hold them (the owners keep their ids private; the defaults are data, looked up in the master lists). The icon address in `TrayService` is the existing repeated string (open item above).

**Not verified (nothing was run).** That Avalonia's `TrayIcon.Clicked` fires on each button-up (so a double click gives two clicks; if it fires once, a double click would open the flyout); that the flyout gets the foreground from a tray click (if Windows refuses, a click-away will not hide it); that a click inside a pick list or the tile row menu does not take the focus from the flyout and hide it; the first-show position on a monitor of another DPI (placed before showing from the measured size, then again); the see-through rounded corners and any Windows 11 frame or shadow on a borderless window; the look in both themes and the tiles' wrap at 4 per row; the slider's binding order with ranges other than 0-100 (a coerced value would be written back); `TrayTests`.

## Developer section: hidden screenshot export (16 Nov) - Debug builds only; compiled only (throwaway Debug and Release builds, 0 errors); never run

For the GitHub showcase images. Lives in `App/Showcase/`, which the csproj leaves out of every non-Debug build (the hooks in `Program.cs`, `App.axaml.cs`, `AppDataStore.cs` are `#if DEBUG`); a Release dll holds no trace (checked for the type names and the flag strings). No CHANGELOG line.
- **Run it:** `bin\<your debug folder>\OmniControl.exe --export-screenshots <folder>` (Debug build). Starts the window off screen, no tray and no start-up entry, writes `<folder>\light\` and `<folder>\dark\` as `NN-<page>[-<view>][-<tab>][-min].png` (`-min` = the minimum window size, no suffix = the default size, 2x pixels; a page with an Easy and an Advanced view, found by a settable `IsEasy` flag on the page, is taken as `-easy` once and `-advanced` with each of its tabs, so Volume gives `-easy`, `-advanced-mixer`, `-advanced-wiring`), then `<NN>-<window>[-min].png` for each window, exits 0 (1 and `export-error.txt` in the folder on failure), and writes `<folder>\privacy-report.txt`.
- **Your own setup (`--real-data`):** `OmniControl.exe --export-screenshots <folder> --real-data`. `ShowcaseRealData` copies the saved files (the `StoreKind.Saved` stores of `DataStores`: settings, mixer, presets, hotkeys, mouse, devices, rules, automation...) from the real `%APPDATA%\OmniControl` into the temporary folder, and the run uses only that copy (deleted on exit; nothing is written to the real folder). Logs, undo copies and the safety files (effects journal, pointer and button-swap originals, display recovery) are not copied, so no start-up recovery can touch Windows. Windows Credential Manager is never read or copied (`NoCredentialStore`: every device shows no saved password). `StartupMode.ReadOnly` (variable `OMNICONTROL_SHOWCASE_REAL`) is the read-only level above `Inert`: `MayRead` lets a page's `OnShown` run, the Displays page ask the monitors (DDC/CI reads, `RefreshNow`) and the mixer scan the audio devices and programs; a saved volume, mute or device is not put back on a running program, and the Devices page's probes and auto-checks, the touchpad takeover, the warm-light scheduler, the mouse hook, hotkeys, the effects engine, automation, the OSD watchers and the emulator keeper stay off. Device states shown are those saved in `devices.json`.
- **Privacy in your own setup:** device, monitor and app names are kept; user, machine and profile names, other accounts' `C:\Users\...` paths, emails, IPs and MACs are replaced as below. A text built from inline runs (which the replacement cannot reach) that still holds such text is blurred (`BlurEffect`, a temporary value like the replacements), and a text box holding it is hidden behind dots. `privacy-report.txt` lists, per picture, what was replaced, blurred or hidden, by kind and count only (never the words). Not covered: text inside images, a name of another kind (an SSID, a document title) that no pattern knows, tooltips and pop-ups (not captured). Look at the pictures before publishing them.
- **Chord:** Ctrl+Alt+Shift+F12 in a running Debug build does the same into `Pictures\OmniControl showcase\<date>` with the owner's own data (windows that create data, the trigger editor and the FX window, are skipped); the footer says where the pictures went. The window size, page, tab and theme are put back afterwards.
- **No settings change, no personal data:** the flag run points `AppDataStore.Folder` at an empty temporary folder (`OMNICONTROL_SHOWCASE_DATA`, set in `Main` before anything reads a setting, deleted on exit), so it shows factory defaults and writes nothing to the real `%APPDATA%\OmniControl`. `ShowcaseScrub` covers texts only while a picture is rendered (a temporary value over the real one, never written to a binding): audio and monitor names become "Desk speakers", "Headphones", "Desk lamp"..., the user and machine names "user", emails `you@example.com`, IPs `192.168.0.10`, MACs `00:11:22:33:44:55`; a text box that holds such text is masked.
- **Lists come from the shell:** pages from `_allPages` and the footer, tabs from each page's own `PageTabs` property (found by type, so a new tabbed page needs nothing), windows from `Showcase/ShellViewModel.Showcase.cs` (`ShowcaseWindows`: Actions and hotkeys, About, Presets, App lists, the trigger editor, the Effects window for the master, the tray quick panel, the screenshot area editor). A new window type is one line there; a window that does not simply close gives a `Finish`. The tray quick panel is the real `TrayFlyoutWindow` over the live master lists (the shell now always makes the `TrayFlyoutModel`; `TrayService` no longer has a no-flyout path). The area editor (`Showcase/ShowcaseCapture.cs`) is the real `CaptureOverlay` over a made-up gradient picture (the screen is never captured) with a sample selection and a box, an arrow, a blur and a text drawn through the view-model's own pointer calls; nothing is saved or copied. App settings is a page, so it is in the page list.
- **Limits / unverified:** the shell still starts its services (mouse hook, audio watchers, global hotkeys at defaults) in the export run, there is no hardware-free shell yet; `Settle` (700 ms) is a dev-only constant in `ShowcaseExporter` (not in `Timing.cs`, which Release compiles). Pop-ups and tooltips are not captured; a TextBlock built from inlines is blurred, not replaced. The area editor overlay makes itself full screen when it opens and the tray panel places itself beside the tray, so each may flash on a real screen for a moment before the exporter moves it away; the export reads `IsEasy` and `PageTabs` by reflection (one `IsEasy` flag, no hand list). Not run by the agent, so the real-data run, the blur and the new windows are unseen. The trigger editor uses the Gestures "Add rule" editor (the only "trigger" window found); the Choose-the-action window in pick mode is not exported (it needs a card to open from). Nothing was run, so offscreen sizes and the placeholder names are unseen.

## Triggers from states and actions (16 Nov) - built to `docs/mockups/triggers-states-actions.html`; compiled only (throwaway builds of the app and the test project, 0 errors, no new warnings); tests written, not run; nothing launched, clicked or switched on the PC

**Audit of the triggers (before building).** Every place something starts a rule or a gesture, and where its list lives:

| Trigger list | Where | Was hand-written? | Now |
|---|---|---|---|
| Automation fixed triggers (log in, OmniControl starts, time of day, timer, idle, wakes, on battery, plugged in, program starts / closes, USB connects / disconnects, hotkey) | `Automation/AutomationTriggers.cs` | Yes, twice: the picker list in `Choices` and again as a `switch` in `PhraseOf` (names and phrases of the same entries in two places) | ONE table `AutomationTriggers.Table` (id, section, name, keywords, phrase, settings, text hint, per-program name); the picker and the sentence both read it; stored ids unchanged |
| Automation: programs offered for start / close | `ProgramNames.Known` (programs with a window + the Volume page's remembered ones) | No (live) | Unchanged, read through the table's per-program entries |
| Automation: "When a state changes" | new: `AutomationTriggers.Live.cs` over `StateRegistry` | - | Read live, every time the picker opens and while it is open |
| Automation: "When an action runs" | new: `AutomationTriggers.Live.cs` over `HotkeyRegistry.Actions` | - | Read live, same |
| Gesture rule triggers (buttons, wheel, tilt, holds, trackpad gestures, shortcut) | `Rules/RuleTriggers.All` | No: one table read by the picker, the phrase, the engine and the conflicts; trackpad entries made from `TrackpadGestures.All` | Unchanged |
| Gesture cards (scroll over an area or with keys held, middle click over an area) | `Gestures/GestureCatalog.Triggers` | No (one master list) | Unchanged |
| Mouse and Trackpad inputs | `MouseButton` values, `TrackpadGestures.All` | No | Unchanged |
| "Only with a hotkey" filter in the picker | `Picker/PickerMenu.axaml` + `PickerModel.Manage.cs` | Shown in every picker window since the keys part was given to pick mode (also triggers and conditions) | Shown only for a list of actions (`PickerModel.ListsActions` / `ShowsKeyFilter`; Noun `PickerModel.ActionNoun`), and the filter is ignored for any other list |

Overlap left on purpose: the fixed "When it starts running on battery" / "When it is plugged in" triggers do what "On battery turns on / turns off" does; both kept so saved rules keep working. Owner to decide whether the fixed pair should go.

**What was built.**
- `States/StateRegistry.cs` + `StateEntry.cs` (`StateEntry`, `StateKind`, `StateValue`, `StateChange`): the master list of states. Owners: `DeviceService.States.cs` (each device's power, `devices.<id>.power`, from `DeviceRuntime.IsOn`/`KnownAt`, notified on every `DeviceRuntime.Changed`: commands, Check now, auto-check), `VolumePageViewModel.States.cs` (`volume.master.level`, `volume.master.muted` from the master row, `volume.default.speaker` from the mixer's devices), `DisplaysPageViewModel.States.cs` (every level of `DisplayControls.Hardware` per attached display, `displays.<display>.<key>`, registered while attached, from the cards), `MouseService.States.cs` (`mouse.layer.mouse`, `mouse.layer.trackpad`, by layer names), `Automation/PowerStates.cs` (`power.battery`, `power.battery.level`, `power.mode`; Core `PowerState.BatteryPercent`, `PowerCommands.CurrentMode` are new). Software display levels are not registered (only the hardware ones were asked for).
- `HotkeyRegistry.RunNow`: the one run path (`Run`, `Run` with settings, `RunLevel`, every key) now raises `Ran` after the gate and the run. A rule's worker puts its `RunChain` in force (`RunWithin`, an `AsyncLocal` instance field of the registry: no static state), it is read on the calling thread and put back around the run on the UI thread, so the action's run, a "Run: rule" it starts and a state change it makes synchronously all carry it. `StateRegistry` reads it at `Notify` (`chainNow`).
- Loops: `RunChain.MayStart` (not in the chain, chain shorter than `Timing.AutomationChainMax` = 3); used by `AutomationService.StartFrom` (state and action triggers) and `RunByAction` (a rule's own action). The minimum gap and burst pause still apply. A state changed asynchronously (a Windows audio callback, a monitor read on a worker) arrives without the chain; such loops are stopped by the minimum gap and the burst pause only.
- Picker: `PickerModel.Live.cs` (`Follow(LiveList)`); the Automation trigger slot follows `AutomationService.TriggersChanged` (raised, coalesced to one per batch, when `HotkeyRegistry.Actions` or `StateRegistry` change). The state entries' settings: a "Now: value" heading with "read HH:mm · how it's known"; on/off "When it" turns on / turns off / changes; level "When it" goes above / goes below / becomes + Value (the state's range and unit); choice "Becomes" + the state's choices (first row "(choose a value)", so the picked value is always stored even when the list order changes).
- Missing: `AutomationService.MissingOf` gives the rule row a caution tag "Trigger missing" (state or action gone: it never fires) or "Action missing" (a step's action gone: `MouseActionRunner` skips it with its existing sentence). The sentence says "a state / an action that is no longer available".
- Merge: `DeviceService.Merge` announces `HotkeyRegistry.Move` (each merged device's On / Off / Toggle -> the same power "via" its first method) and `StateRegistry.Move` (its power -> the target device's). Followers rewrite their own stored text through `Mouse/MovedActions`: `MouseService.FollowMoved` (bindings and extra actions, every layer), `GestureService.FollowMoved`, `RuleService` (steps), `AutomationService.Live` (steps, block actions, "action runs" and "state" triggers). A deleted device's actions and state are simply gone: rules show them missing; buttons and gestures keep their existing "no longer available" behaviour.

**Not done / open.** Layer presets that hold bindings (mouse layer presets saved in the preset manager) are not rewritten on a merge. Renaming a mouse layer changes the choice text a "Mouse layer becomes ..." rule stored, so such a rule then never fires until it is picked again. The "Now:" line of an entry whose settings are open is not refreshed while the window stays open (the list is, on any list change). Not run or seen: the picker sections and settings, the tags, firing on real state changes, the merge rewrite, the chain cut. Tests in `StateTriggerTests.cs` (registry live updates, the trigger maths, loop limits, live list, missing, moved ids) compile but were not run.

## Actions and hotkeys window (16 Nov) - built to `docs/mockups/actions-and-hotkeys.html`; compiled only (throwaway builds of the app and the test project, 0 errors); tests written, not run; nothing launched, clicked or pressed on the PC

**Audit (before building).** Everything the Shortcuts page and the temporary All actions page showed or did, and where it is now:

| Was | Now |
|---|---|
| Shortcuts: title-line `PresetPicker` of the shortcut sets (`HotkeySets`, `HotkeySetPresets` registered as preset group `shortcuts.sets`, "Manage presets…" to the preset manager) | The window's top bar, same picker and group id (`Picker/HotkeySetPresets.cs`, owned by `Picker/ActionsWindowService`); the preset manager lists the group under a section "Actions and hotkeys" (added after the pages in `ShellViewModel`) |
| Shortcuts: "Clear all" (asks once, keeps device choices) | Top bar, same question and behaviour; also clears the extra keys |
| Shortcuts: "Restore defaults" (`SettingsRestore` key `shortcuts`, part of Restore all, its own action `shortcuts.restore`) | Top bar `RestoreBar`; wired by `ShellViewModel.Restore.cs` like a page's; same key and action id (title now "Restore Actions and hotkeys defaults") |
| Shortcuts: folding cards per page, group and subgroup with "N shortcuts · M set" | The picker's sections and group headings (`Section`, `PickerHeading`); the counts are gone |
| Shortcuts: "Use suggested keys" pill per card (bulk) | Per action "Suggest" pill in the right pane; the bulk `HotkeyRegistry.UseSuggested` and `HotkeyDefaults.WithSuggestion` were deleted |
| Shortcuts: a card per action with the `HotkeyBox` and the device scope (`HotkeyRow` selector with its ticked list) | Right pane: one `HotkeyBox` per key, then the same `HotkeyRow` (now the device selector only) |
| Shortcuts: an unavailable action dimmed with its reason | Entry greyed with the reason (`ManageChoices` sets `Unavailable`), read when the window opens (not live while it is open) |
| Shortcuts: devices refreshed when the page was shown | `ActionHotkeys` refreshes the action's devices when its pane is made |
| Shortcuts: search entries "<action> hotkey" with the live hotkey row | One search entry per action with an "Open" button that opens the window at that action (`ActionsWindowService.SearchEntries`) |
| Shortcuts: `KeyHelp` text | Dropped (the window's keys follow the picker's; no on-screen help, principle 12) |
| All actions: search, sections and headings like the picker, Run, the `HotkeyBox`, "Show on its page" | All in the window (Run now, Show on its page = `ShellViewModel.OpenPage`) |

Master list: every entry is read live from `HotkeyRegistry.PickerActions` through `MouseActionCatalog.ManageChoices` (the same `OmniChoice`/`OmniEntry` every picker uses); the keys from `HotkeyAction.Keys`. No hand-made list was found; the Shortcuts page's group tree (`HotkeyGroup`) was only a view of the registry and is deleted with the page.

**Built.** `Pages/Shortcuts/` and `Pages/AllActions/` deleted with their DataTemplates, the `HotkeyAction` -> `HotkeyRow` template, `NavGroups.Pinned`, `HotkeyAction.Panel` and the shell's pinned-page list; the sidebar's bottom list holds one `Shell/NavLauncher` ("Actions and hotkeys", click, Enter or Space opens the window, the entry does not stay selected). Several keys per action: `HotkeyAction.MoreKeys`/`Keys`/`SetKeys`, `StoredHotkey.More` (old hotkeys.json, shortcut sets and backups have no `More` and read as one key; nothing is rewritten until a key changes), `HotkeyRegistry.Unique` keeps keys unique on load and on a set switch (a merged backup can bring a key twice: the first action keeps it), `OnChanged` takes the changed action's keys off every other action. Hotkey boxes work on `HotkeyTarget` (an action, or a `Picker/HotkeyKeySlot`). The shared `HotkeyBox` now listens on a double-click or Enter (was a single click), also in the preset manager.

**Choices made (owner may overrule):**
- Pick mode (a mouse button, gesture or rule opening the picker) shows the same keycaps, filter, key search and Hotkey part, plus "Use this action". The callers are unchanged: the window gets the keys part (`ManageMode`, live registry) from `PickerWindow.UseKeys`, a set-once static given by `ActionsWindowService` at start-up. This is the one hard-coded exception (a static), taken so no picker caller is handed the master list.
- While the window is in front and its search box or a hotkey box has the keys, every key except those of `NeverBlock` actions is unregistered from Windows (`HotkeyRegistry.Hold`), so a pressed key reaches the box instead of running its action; the emergency key Ctrl+Alt+Shift+P has its own registration and always works. Applies in both modes. They come back when focus leaves those boxes or the window.
- Search by pressing: a key with Ctrl, Alt or Win, or an F key or Print Screen alone, fills the search box with it; Ctrl+F and the text box's own editing keys (Ctrl+A/C/V/X/Z/Y, Ctrl+arrows, Ctrl+Backspace/Delete/Home/End, with or without Shift) are kept for editing (`HotkeyCapture.SearchChordOf`).
- The search box's "(Ctrl+F)" is in its tooltip, not its watermark (principle 12).
- The devices selector sits on the "+ add key" line (the spec has no place for it; it was on every Shortcuts card).
- Enter or a double-click on an entry goes to its first key box (nothing is "used" in manage mode). Keyboard in the hotkey part: arrows walk its boxes and pills (wrapping), Down from the last goes on to the settings, Tab or Esc back to the list; mouse-first otherwise (rule 5).
- After a key is given, the key boxes are rebuilt and the keys go back to the box of the key just set (`ActionHotkeys.KeyToFocus`).

**Unverified:** nothing was run or seen. Not checked: the look at the narrowest window (`MinWidth` 720) with long names plus keycaps, both themes, the hold of the keys, capture of a chord that another app owns, a merged backup with clashing keys.

## Area video recording, text colour and sizes (16 Nov) - built to `docs/mockups/area-video-recording.html` and the additions in `screenshot-toolbars-a.html`; compiled only (throwaway builds of Core, the app and the test project, 0 errors); `VideoRecordingTests` written, not run; nothing recorded, captured or shown on the PC

- **Actions:** the area overlay has a red Record button after Save (`Button.toolicon.danger`, "Record video"); new master action "Record area" (`screenshot.record`, `ShotKind.Record`, section Screenshot, search words record/video/mp4/gif) opens the same overlay and starts recording when the drag that draws the selection ends. While a recording runs, the area action and "Record area" stop it (any of their hotkeys); the window/screen/all-screens screenshots still work. Drawings are not recorded.
- **Recording state:** the overlays close (the frozen pictures are released).
