## Easy Sound drawer follows the real effect (14 Nov 2026) - compiled (Release throwaway build, 0 warnings, 0 errors, folder deleted) and `dotnet test` 354/354 pass (342 + 12 new in `EasyShapeTests`); nothing run, seen, clicked or heard; no Windows or audio setting changed; the running app was not stopped

**Cause (PROVEN from the code, not seen on screen):** the chart itself was already the right one (`FxAnalysisViewModel` -> `ChainResponse.Of`, the FX header's own function, same chain list, same scale). What was wrong was that the guided setup's Sound screen builds its own `EasySound` (`EasyViewModel.NewMasterSound`) and nothing refreshed it: `EasyViewModel.Refresh` only reached the page's `Master.Sound` and the app rows. So that curve was drawn once when the screen opened (the low shelf only) and the FX window's later edits (the +17 dB bell) never reached it. Edits made in the FX window do raise `MixerService.FxChanged` through `SaveFx` on every change.
**Fix:** every `EasySound` now listens to `MixerService.FxChanged` for its own chain key (and `EffectsEngine.PlanChanged`) and unsubscribes in `Dispose`, so each drawer (master, each app, each microphone, the setup's Sound screen) follows the FX window live; sliders, chips, curve and notes are re-read. `Commit` no longer calls `Refresh` itself (the event does). No change to Advanced or the shared FX files.
**Sliders and the real effect:** `EasyEq.Matches(bands)` = five bands with the Easy types, frequencies within 1 % and Qs within 0.01, all on, gains inside +-12 dB. `Describes(slot)` also needs no overall gain and a 100 % wet mix. While the effect is described, the sliders follow the real gains in both directions. Otherwise (`IsCustom`: a band that does something, or an overall gain, outside that layout) the five sliders are greyed (`EasySound.SlidersAvailability`), a line under the chips says "Shaped in the full effects window" (tooltip: why) with a "Back to simple sliders" link; "My own" stays selected; chart and chips keep working. A flat effect in another layout has no shape to lose and is simply rewritten. **Confirm before replacing:** a preset chip, the hotkey "Next sound preset" and "Back to simple sliders" ask first ("Use simple sliders" / Cancel keeps the shape; the shared message window's cancel button is always "Cancel", so the owner's "Keep my shape" wording is in the sentence instead). "Back" puts in `EasyEq.BestFit`: the effect's whole response (bands, overall gain, wet) sampled at the five frequencies, clamped to +-12 dB, so the sound changes least. `EasyEq.Write` now also resets the overall gain and wet so the result is exactly the Easy layout. The guided "Apply my sound" step (`EasyApply.MasterSound`) only switches a custom shape on and never rewrites it.
**Open items:** the microphone look step (`EasyApply.Microphone`) still replaces a microphone's equaliser without asking (a custom shape on a microphone is lost; the look step asks nothing today). Sliders edit only the chain's first Parametric equaliser while the chart draws every enabled equaliser and Gain of the chain, so another enabled equaliser changes the sound without greying the sliders. A band switched off in the FX window counts as not matching. Slider moves rewrite all five gains as whole dB (a fractional gain set in the FX window on another band is rounded when a slider moves).
**Owner test:** (1) Master sound screen or drawer open, open the master's FX window, change a band's gain: the Easy chart moves with it, and the Easy sliders follow while the layout is Easy. (2) Change a band's frequency (or add or remove a band, change its type, or set a gain beyond 12 dB): the five sliders grey out and "Shaped in the full effects window" appears; the chart still matches the FX header. (3) Pick a chip or press "Back to simple sliders": a confirm appears; Cancel changes nothing; "Use simple sliders" replaces the effect (the FX window shows five bands). (4) Same in an app's drawer.

## Easy guided setups: the Engine step (14 Nov 2026; built to `docs/mockups/easy-engine-choice.html`) - compiled (Release throwaway build, 0 warnings, 0 errors, folder deleted) and `dotnet test` 369/369 pass (354 + 15 new in `EasyEngineTests`; 5 `EasyFlowTests` adjusted); nothing run, seen, clicked or heard; no Windows or audio setting changed; the running app was not stopped; no installer or mode switch was called (tests use fakes)

**Hard rule kept:** no Advanced file was edited (Easy files, `Pages/Volume/Easy/**`, and tests only).

**As built**
- **Where the rule lives, once:** `EasyGoal.Engine` (an `EasyEngineRule`: `ApoWhyNot`, `CableWhyNot`, one facts line per card) in `EasyGoals`. Goals 1 to 3 offer both ways (goal 2's and 3's facts lines say what the choice means: master by APO on the speakers or through the cable bus, apps recorded either way; the microphone on its device by APO, or through a cable in Full control); goals 4 and 5 offer only the cable ("Joining microphones needs a virtual cable", "Several speakers need a virtual cable": the APO card is greyed, not selectable, its reason replaces its facts line, its Get button is off); goal 6 has no rule and so no step. The step is not listed in `EasyFlows.All`: `EasyFlows.StepsOf(goal, def)` inserts it before "Setup" when the goal has a rule, so the strip is Goal, questions, Engine, Setup, Done and a goal without a rule keeps its own.
- **Live cards:** `EasyFacts` gained `CablesReady` (allowed pairs) and `CableInstalled`; `EasyEnvironment` fills them from `SoundModes.AllowedPairs` / `CableInstalled`. `EasyEngines.Cards` makes both cards from the facts (tag "Installed" good / "Not installed" caution; "1 cable ready" / "N cables ready" good / "Cables switched off" / "No virtual cable installed" caution) and `EasyEngineItem` updates in place on every `Reevaluate`, so an install finishing turns the tag good, greys the Get button and lifts the Next gate by itself. Get buttons run the existing `EasyFixes.InstallApo` / `InstallCable` steps (the page's own installer code), only when pressed.
- **Pre-selection** (`EasyEngines.Default`): the only way a goal offers; else Equalizer APO, unless only a cable is installed (then the cable); nothing installed gives Equalizer APO. It is settled once on the first read and then kept (a later install does not move it) and is not "remembered". A user pick is reported through the flow's `engineChosen` callback; `EasyViewModel` keeps it per goal for the session and hands it to the next flow of that goal ("Set up again"). Not saved to disk (open item if wanted).
- **Next** is off while the chosen way is not there, with one plain sentence under the cards and as the Next tooltip ("Install Equalizer APO first.", "Install a virtual cable first.", "Cables switched off. Switch one on under Virtual cables in Advanced."). Both cards stay selectable in every install state (except a goal's refused one). Back from Setup returns to the step with the pick kept.
- **The choice drives the Setup checklist** (`EasyChecks`, keyed on `EasyAnswers.Engine`; null counts as Equalizer APO):
  - Better: APO = installed, speakers in Device Selector, apply (plus "Switch to Fast mode" when Full control still has the master on the cable bus, after its question); Cable = one line "OmniControl's engine runs your sound" (done when Full control and the master on the bus; otherwise the confirmed switch to Full control, or "Use the cable" without a question when already in Full control with the master's method on Equalizer APO; blocked with the reason when no cable is allowed), then apply. The old optional "Use another way" line is gone: the Engine step replaces it.
  - A different sound per app: same engine line first (Cable) or the Fast-mode line when needed (APO); then the existing "effects work" and "own sound" lines.
  - Microphone: APO = as before; Cable = Full control line, a free cable line, then Apply (no Device Selector lines).
  - Goals 4 and 5: unchanged checklists (they already needed the cable).

**Differences from the spec**
- The Get button sits directly under its card, not inside it (a button inside the card button would be a nested button for mouse and keyboard); the card is a `Button.optioncard`, the Get button a `Button.pill.cardaction`; tags `Border.tag` `.good` / `.caution`.
- "Master method Cable bus" is done by leaving the method Automatic (the existing `UseOwnEngine` only changes an Equalizer APO method back to Automatic); no explicit `MasterBus` method is written. Done is judged by the live `MasterOnBus`.
- With the APO way, a PC that is in Full control with the master on the bus gets a "Switch to Fast mode" line (otherwise the sound would not run in Equalizer APO). The spec said the master method is irrelevant; it is irrelevant when the master is already off the bus.

**Not verified / open items**
- Nothing seen on screen: the two-card layout, greyed look of a refused card (`IsEnabled`), tag colours and wrapping at the narrowest window, both themes.
- The microphone Cable path reuses the combined-device cable rule ("a free cable"); whether a single microphone effect should count a cable as taken the same way is not checked against the planner.
- "Get a virtual cable" runs the page's VB-CABLE installer step (the same as Advanced's); "More cables" is not offered on this step.
- The remembered pick is per session only.

**Owner test steps**
1. Set up again, "Make everything sound better", pick a sound, Next: the Engine step shows both cards; with Equalizer APO installed its tag is good and "Get Equalizer APO" is greyed.
2. Pick the other card, press Next: the checklist changes (cable: one engine line; APO: installed, speakers, apply). Back returns to the Engine step with your pick.
3. "Join two microphones" and "Play on two speakers": the Equalizer APO card is greyed with its reason and cannot be picked.
4. With a way missing, Next is dimmed with one sentence; install it (button) and the tag turns good, the button greys and Next turns on without leaving the screen.
5. Open "Set up again" for the same goal: your last pick is selected.

## Easy page: "Restore defaults" (14 Nov 2026) - compiled (Release throwaway build, 0 warnings, 0 errors, folder deleted) and `dotnet test` 405/405 pass (393 + 12 new in `AudioDefaultsTests`); nothing run, seen, clicked or heard; no Windows or audio setting changed; the running app was not stopped

**Hard rule kept:** no Advanced control changed. The only shared file touched is `VolumePage.axaml`: the title row became a `Grid` (title, switch, then the new button at the right, `IsVisible` = Easy), so Advanced's title line looks the same.

- **Button:** `Button.pill` "Restore defaults" at the right of the title row (Easy only), greyed with the tooltip "Already at the defaults" when `AudioDefaults.IsAtDefaults` (a pure comparison of exactly the settings the restore covers) is true; it is re-read on every `Refresh`. It opens ONE confirm (the Easy `EasyConfirm` window) with the owner's wording (plus one sentence about combined devices when any exist: they stay but are inactive in Fast mode), buttons Restore / Cancel.
- **What it does** (`Audio/AudioDefaults.cs`): first saves a copy of the mixer settings to `%APPDATA%\OmniControl\mixer-before-restore.json`; then master, every program, every microphone and every combined microphone get effects OFF (chains kept as data), the master's Easy-layout equaliser is made flat (a shape made in the FX window is kept, not flattened), every program's effects route goes to Automatic and its "plays on" is cleared, the master's "plays on" is cleared, the master method is Automatic, the remembered Full control setup is "none yet" (= setup 1), all cables are allowed (switched-off and freed lists emptied), and the sound mode is Fast. The mode is changed last through `MixerService.SetSoundMode`, so the engine stops the bus, puts Windows' default playback device back (BusDefault) and clears OmniControl's Equalizer APO lines by its own existing paths; nothing writes APO files or devices directly. Volumes, mutes, balance, the program list, combined devices, marked cables, the capture-test result and device names are untouched.
- **Undo:** the confirmation line shows "Audio defaults restored" and an `Undo` link (`Button.link small`) for `Timing.RestoreUndo` (60 s); any other confirmation, or the time running out, removes the link. Undo applies the copy through the same `Apply` and so touches only what the restore touched: a level changed after the restore stays. Missing copy: "Nothing to undo: the saved copy was not found."
- **Hotkey/search:** "Restore audio defaults" (`volume.easy.restore`, keywords "reset default audio restore ..."); it asks the same question; at the defaults it says so on the Easy line.

**Differences / not done / unverified**
- The sound mode goes through `MixerService.SetSoundMode` (which the engine reacts to), not through `SoundModeService`, which only reads what the PC offers.
- Cancelling the question is a `return` before `Restore` in `EasyViewModel.RestoreDefaultsAsync`; it has no unit test (the view-model needs the UI thread). The restore and undo logic is tested with fakes (what changes, what stays, copy before change, undo exact, at-defaults, no change at defaults).
- If the copy cannot be written (read-only folder), `AppDataStore.Save` stays silent and Undo then says the copy was not found.
- The engine, Windows' default device and Equalizer APO's section were not exercised: they follow the existing mode-change path, unseen here.

**Owner test steps**
1. Easy page with something set (an effect on, or Full control): "Restore defaults" is active; with everything at defaults it is grey and its tooltip says "Already at the defaults".
2. Press it: one window with the sentence; Cancel changes nothing. Restore: effects off on Everything, apps and microphones (Advanced shows their effects off, chains still there), Sound mode Fast, methods Automatic, cables all allowed, Windows' default device back to your speakers, levels and mutes as they were; the line "Audio defaults restored" with Undo.
3. Undo within a minute: effects, mode, methods, routing and cables return; after a minute the link is gone. `mixer-before-restore.json` exists in `%APPDATA%\OmniControl`.
4. Shortcuts page: search "restore audio defaults".


## "Restore defaults" on every page and "Restore all defaults" (14 Nov 2026) - compiled (Release throwaway build, 0 warnings, 0 errors, folder deleted) and `dotnet test` 427/427 pass (407 + 20 new in `RestoreDefaultsTests`); nothing run, seen, clicked or heard; no Windows or app setting changed; the running app was not stopped

**One mechanism** (files in `ARCHITECTURE.md`, "Restore defaults"): a page overrides `PageViewModel.Restorable` with a `SettingsRestore<T>` (what it has now, the defaults, how to put a value in place through its own cards and services); the shell wraps it in a `PageRestore` (`Restorer`) and every title line shows the one `RestoreBar` (`Button.pill` "Restore defaults", the same class as the Volume Easy button, far right of the title line, greyed with its reason as the tooltip, a calm line and an `Undo` link for `Timing.RestoreUndo` = 60 s; `Timing.EasyRestoreUndo` was renamed to it). The question is an `EngineMessage` through `FxWindowService.AskAsync` (as the Easy one). A copy of every page's settings is taken BEFORE the first change, kept in memory and written to `<key>-before-restore.json` (`all-before-restore.json` for Restore all). A hotkey action "Restore <page> defaults" is registered for each page except Volume (its own `volume.easy.restore` stays).

| Page | What is put back | What stays | Greyed when |
|---|---|---|---|
| Displays | every display's software picture (brightness, contrast, gamma, RGB, levels, temperature), saturation, grayscale, invert; warm light off | display presets, warm-light times and temperature, every monitor's own (DDC/CI) settings ("Monitor settings are not changed") | at defaults |
| Volume | `AudioDefaults` (all effects, combined devices, methods, routes and marked cables removed, cables allowed, Fast mode; see "Restore removes" below) | levels, mutes, balance, programs with a non-default value, saved presets, the Easy or Advanced choice | at defaults |
| OSD | every `OsdSettings` value and the Show in OSD switches | nothing | at defaults |
| Mouse and Trackpad | `MouseSettings` (mappings, layers, layer names, options; the mouse service saves and applies, and the trackpad service gives back any Windows gesture no mapping takes over), layers back to 1; pointer settings and pointers back to `pointer-settings-original.json` through `PointerSettingsService.RestoreTo` | pointer settings when OmniControl never changed one | at defaults |
| Gestures | built-in gestures off with default actions, switch on, edge strip default; custom rules and approved commands DELETED | nothing | at defaults; the question gets the line "Deletes your N custom rules" and the button "Restore anyway" when rules exist |
| Shortcuts | every key combination removed, every action on all its devices | nothing | at defaults |
| App settings | theme System, accent Orange, Start with Windows off (through `StartupRegistration`), start minimised off, When closing "Ask each time", user plug-in folders removed | scan results, Volume view, first-run choice, window position, effect-editor layouts | at defaults; "A plug-in scan is running" |

**Restore all:** the App settings card "Restore all defaults" (button "Restore all"), its line and Undo under the cards. One question lists every page's sentence (and the rules line), one combined copy, pages restored in sidebar order with App settings last; pages already at their defaults or unavailable are left out; one that fails is named in one sentence ("Couldn't fully restore X; the rest was restored.") and the others go on; Undo puts them back last first. The Easy or Advanced choice, the first-run decision and the window position are never touched.

**Differences from the brief / decisions**
- The warm-light schedule's times and temperature are kept (the brief said "warm light off" only); the pointer part is skipped when OmniControl never changed a pointer setting.
- Mouse and Gestures: the on/off switch moved from the far right to beside the title (the same place as Volume's Easy | Advanced switch) to leave the far right to Restore defaults.
- Volume: Easy keeps its own button, line and hotkey; Advanced shows the shared button (same `AudioDefaults` scope) with its own line.
- Custom rules: one stronger question (the count in the text and "Restore anyway" on the button), not a second window. A declined question changes nothing, so rules are never deleted without it.
- The button state is looked at once a second while the window is shown (`Timing.RestoreStateCheck`), so it follows edits made on the page; it is also refreshed when the page opens and after a restore or undo.

**Not done / unverified (nothing can be seen or run on this PC)**
- No screen was looked at: the title-line layout at the narrowest window (680 px) with a long line, both themes, and the keyboard order of the button on pages without grouped cards. The title row is not shown in the search-results view, so the button is not either.
- Nothing was written to Windows: the pointer restore, the start-up entry, the gesture takeover hand-back, the display ramps and the OSD pop-ups (the OSD restore shows its usual change preview pop-ups) were not exercised. The pure logic, the copy order, the combined restore, the failure line and the rules question are tested with fakes (`RestoreDefaultsTests`). The page glue (`*PageViewModel.Restore.cs`) has no test (it needs the UI thread and the services).
- If a copy cannot be written (read-only folder) `AppDataStore.Save` stays silent; Undo still works in the same run, from memory.
- The Shortcuts page and the mouse and gesture pickers now list one "Restore <page> defaults" action per page (the master list).
- While the Mouse page is open and OmniControl has a pointer backup, the pointer values and pointers are read once a second to grey the button (cheap system calls).

**Owner test steps**
1. Every page: change a value; the title line's "Restore defaults" turns on; at the defaults it is grey with "Already at the defaults".
2. Press it: one window naming what is restored and what stays; Cancel changes nothing; Restore does it, a line appears with "Undo" for a minute, Undo brings the old values back.
3. Gestures with a custom rule: the window says "Deletes your 1 custom rule" with "Restore anyway"; after it the rule is gone, Undo brings it back.
4. Displays: set brightness and warm light on; restore: pictures neutral, warm light off, presets still there, the monitor's own sliders unchanged.
5. Mouse and Trackpad: map a button, change pointer speed; restore: mapping gone, speed back to what you had before OmniControl first changed it.
6. App settings: change theme, accent, start minimised; "Restore all defaults" lists every page; Undo restores them all.
7. Shortcuts page: search "restore"; each page has its action.


## Version line and App settings pill (14 Nov)
- The version has one master: `<Version>` in `OmniControl.App.csproj`, read by `Services/AppInfo.cs`. The sidebar's grey line under the title shows `AppInfo.VersionText`; the About window title uses `AppInfo.NameAndVersion`. `AppInfoTests` scans the app's source for the version in a string. tools/release.ps1 reads the same property for the Setup and Portable pre-release; CHANGELOG.md holds the notes.
- The footer's left text is gone; App settings is now a `Button.pill` (gear icon + words) at the footer's left before "Keyboard shortcuts", accent look (`.custom`) while the page is open (`PageViewModel.IsOpen`, set by the shell). The sidebar's App settings list was removed.
- Keys: Up/Down in the category list still wrap, now through the footer button (Down from the last page lands on App settings and opens it, Down from there goes to the first page; Up mirrors). Tab still only swaps sidebar and page (it never reached the footer buttons). Not verified on screen.

## Mouse remapping is always on (14 Nov)
- Removed the "Remap mouse and trackpad" switch, `MouseSettings.Enabled`, `MouseService.SetEnabled`/`StateChanged`, the hook's enabled gate, the `Enabled` checks in `TrackpadService` (takeover and safety net) and `RuleConflicts`, and the hotkey `mouse.toggle` ("Toggle mouse remapping"; a stored hotkey or button mapping for it falls under the existing "no longer available" handling). Inputs at Default keep Windows' behaviour.
- Old `mouse.json`: the stored `Enabled` value is ignored on load (System.Text.Json skips unknown members) and never written again.
- The Mouse and Trackpad page no longer has its own header: the shell's title line shows the name and "Restore defaults" at the far right (`ShowsOwnHeader` dropped). Not run or seen; the owner checks the page and search.

## Volume "Restore defaults" now REMOVES (14 Nov 2026) - compiled (Release throwaway build, 0 warnings, 0 errors, folder deleted), `dotnet test` 429/429 pass; nothing run, seen or clicked; no setting changed; the running app was not stopped

**Restore removes** (`Audio/AudioDefaults.cs`; Easy button, Advanced title-row button, hotkey `volume.easy.restore` and Restore all): every effect chain of the master, each program and each microphone (chains emptied, switches off: rows read "no effects"; the master EQ data goes with the master chain), all combined microphones and speakers (through `MixerService.RemoveCombined`, so their engine run stops and "plays on" references are cleaned), every program's method, plays-on and old device override, the master's method and plays-on, hand-marked cables (`MixerService.SetMarkedCables`), switched-off cables (all allowed), the remembered Full control setup, and the sound mode (Fast). The program "added" mark is dropped (`MixerService.SetAdded`), so a program that then holds only defaults is unlisted by `AppAudioEntry.KeepsRow`; volumes, mutes and balance are kept, so such programs stay listed. Saved user presets are a separate store and are not touched. A copy is still saved first (`mixer-before-restore.json`) and Undo restores chains, combined devices (re-added with their effects, levels and members), routes, marks and the added mark through the same `Apply`. Wording (one source, `AudioDefaults.Scope`, used by the Easy confirm, the Volume page scope and the tooltip): "Removes all effects (master, apps, microphones), all combined devices and every method and route you set, allows all virtual cables and uses Fast mode. Your volume levels and mutes stay." plus "You can undo this." "Already at the defaults" now compares against no effects, no combined devices, defaults everywhere.

Unverified: the real engine reaction (each combined device removal replans the engine once, then the mode change); a combined device re-added by Undo gets a cable again only when the engine plans it. Input entries (name, last cable) are kept, only their chains are emptied.

Owner test steps: 1. With effects on a program, the microphone and the master, a combined mic and a combined speaker, press Restore defaults, confirm: rows read "no effects", the combined devices are gone from Wiring and from every "plays on", Fast mode is on. 2. Undo (within 60 s): chains and combined devices are back. 3. A program with a changed volume or mute stays listed; an added program with defaults only disappears from the closed list. 4. Right after restoring the button is grey.

## Unified preset manager (14 Nov 2026) - compiled (Release throwaway build, folder deleted) and `dotnet test` 480/480 pass (453 + 27 new in `PresetManagerTests`, one `MonitorPresetTests` case rewritten for the new card); nothing run or seen; no setting of the owner's changed (the tests use fakes and in-memory stores); the running app was not stopped

Built to `docs/mockups/unified-preset-manager.html`. ONE window, **Presets** (`Pages/Presets/PresetManagerWindow`), replaces the layer manager, the Displays presets window and the FX preset manager (all three removed with their models, rows and the FX name dialog).

**Master list.** `App/Presets/PresetGroups.Register(new PresetGroup(id, section, title, provider))`: section = the page's `Title`, title = the group's name on its page. Registered: Displays page `displays.hardware` "Hardware" (monitor presets) and `displays.software` "Software" (picture presets); Mouse and Trackpad page `mouse.buttons` "Mouse Buttons" and `mouse.trackpad` "Trackpad" (layers); Volume: one group per effect kind with presets (`volume.fx.<kind>`, titled with the kind's catalog name: "Equaliser - Parametric", "Equaliser - 32 Bands", "Equaliser - 16 Bands", "Compressor"), registered by the shell from `FxWindowService.PresetGroupsFor(volume title)`. Ids are part of the shortcut action ids: never rename. A new group is one `Register` call; the window, its sections and the shortcuts follow.

**Provider = adapter of the existing model** (`IPresetProvider`: entries with a stable id, in use, built-in, tags, what can be done; add, rename, move, delete with undo, duplicate, export/import, apply, a group switch). `LayerPresets` (over `ILayerControl` = `MouseService`): the layers the drop-down lists; apply = switch to; add = name the next unlisted layer; delete = clear its mappings and its name (the always-listed layers, mouse 1 and trackpad 1-3, are only cleared: "Clear"); undo through `LayerSet.Snapshot` / `Restore`; the "Only named or used layers" switch is on the card. `BookPresets` (over `IPresetBook`): picture and monitor presets, monitors as tags (connected ones green, others "(not connected)"); add saves the live settings as "Picture" / "Monitors" (then "... 2"); greyed with "No monitor controls to save" when no monitor reports one. `FxKindPresets` (over `UserPresetStore` + `PresetCatalog`): built-ins first (tagged "built-in", never renamed, moved or deleted; duplicate and shortcut allowed), the user's own after; reserved names, copy names, import/export of a .json file, delete with undo, as before.

**Stable ids (new, backward compatible).** `DisplayPreset`, `MonitorPreset` (`INamedPreset.Id`) and `UserPreset.Id`: an older file's presets get an id once on load and the file is saved once; replacing a preset by name keeps its id; an exported preset file carries no id. Layers use their number.

**Which effect a Volume preset reaches** (apply, shortcut, "Save current"): the selected effect of the FX window used last when it is of that kind; else the master's first effect of that kind (through the master's FX window when it is open, so its editor shows it; else written into the master chain directly, `PresetCatalog.Write`, hand-tuned settings kept as Custom, saved and sent to the engine); with none, nothing changes and the window's line says "No Equaliser - Parametric to use: add one to the master, or select one in an FX window." (the same sentence on the footer for a shortcut).

**Shortcuts per preset** (`App/Presets/PresetShortcuts`). The row's shortcut box is the shared `Hotkeys/HotkeyBox` (extracted from `HotkeyRow`, which now uses it; width `HotkeyBoxWidth` 150 as before): click (or Enter) and press keys (earlier: double-click); Backspace removes. A key is refused, with the reason on the window's line, when it is unreadable, has no Ctrl, Alt or Win (not an F key), or another action has it (`HotkeyRegistry.ConflictOf`; the Shortcuts page still moves a key silently, as before). A given key makes the action `preset.<group id>.<preset id>` "Apply preset "Evening"" (layers: "Switch to layer "Laptop"") under the page, the group and "Presets" (Shortcuts: "Displays - Software - Presets"), so it is searchable, on the Shortcuts page and in the mouse pickers. The key is saved in hotkeys.json by that id. Renaming the preset re-makes the action with the new title (same id and key); deleting the preset removes the action (its key stays saved, so Undo brings it back); removing the key in the manager removes the action; a key cleared on the Shortcuts page leaves the action until the next start. At start-up every preset with a saved key gets its action when its group registers.

**Opening** (`PresetManagerService.Open(section, groupId)`, the one opener; non-modal, owned by the main window, opened again = brought to the front and moved): Mouse Buttons cogwheel -> Mouse and Trackpad / Mouse Buttons; Trackpad cogwheel -> Trackpad; Displays Hardware / Software cogwheels -> their cards; the FX preset box's "Manage presets..." -> Volume / that effect's card; App settings has a new "Presets" card (Open) -> the first section with presets (Displays). The target's section is chosen, its card outlined (`Border.groupPanel.target`) and scrolled into view.

**Window.** Left: the sidebar's pages in order with their icons and "N groups"; pages without presets (OSD, Gestures, Shortcuts) dimmed "none", not selectable. Right: the section's name, a search box (name or shortcut, every section; cards then titled "Page - Group"), one card per group: rows = drag grip (drag to reorder; Alt+Up/Down), name (double-click, F2 or Rename: in place; Enter or leaving the box applies, Esc cancels), "in use" tag, tags, shortcut box, Apply / Switch to (hidden on the one in use), Rename, Delete / Clear, and a "..." menu where offered (Duplicate, Export to a file...); the card's foot: dashed "+ Save the current ... as a preset" / "+ Add a layer" (it then opens the new row's name box), Import... (effects), the layers' switch. Foot of the window: "Changes apply at once.", one message line (a refusal), "Deleted "X"" + Undo for `Timing.PresetUndo`, Close. Mouse first (rule 5 allowance, owner can overrule): Tab reaches everything, the card keys of `RowEditing` work in each card, Ctrl+F searches, Esc ends a drag, clears the search, then closes.

**Start-up order** (`ShellViewModel`, written in its constructor): `_presetGroups` (field) -> `PresetShortcuts` -> `PresetManagerService` -> FX windows (given `Open`) -> pages (register their groups after their own actions) -> the effects' groups -> `UseSections` (the pages). The window's model is made only when the window opens. `PresetManagerTests.BuildingEveryPieceInTheShellsOrder_WithNoData_Works` builds every piece in that order with no displays, no monitors, no presets and no layers named.

**Differences from the mock-up.** The shortcut is the shared hotkey box (watermark "Double-click to set", a key text when set), not a dashed "+ shortcut" chip, so there is one capture box in the program; the dragged row is lifted (drawn above the others, as in the FX chain), not outlined; the target card's accent edge is 1 px, not 2 px (nothing moves); the Volume cards are named with the effects' catalog names ("Equaliser - Parametric"), the one name used everywhere; a section shows "N groups" (not "effects"); Duplicate and Export sit in a small "..." menu so a row fits at the narrowest window (720 px).

**Changed behaviour (said plainly).** Save current no longer asks for a name in a dialog: it saves under a first free name and opens the row's name box. Renaming to a name another preset has is refused ("A preset of this name exists."); the FX manager used to ask "Replace it?". The FX manager's multi-selection, Move up/Move down buttons, Ctrl+A and "Show in list" are gone (drag or Alt+Up/Down one row; search shows the card). The layer window's "Done" is the window's Close. The mouse layer window was modal; this window is not.

**Open items.** (1) Layers cannot be reordered: "Hold for layer N", "Switch to layer N", the custom rules' layer and gestures.json refer to layer numbers; moving a layer would need all of them rewritten (the grip is hidden on layer rows, the card says why on a drag). (2) A mouse button or gesture mapped to an "Apply preset" action points to nothing after its key is removed in the manager (the action is gone; it comes back when a key is given again). (3) Undoing a monitor preset's delete does not bring back which monitor had it last (`MonitorLastPreset`). (4) "In use" on an effect card follows a change made in the manager or a store change, not every slider move in an FX window. (5) The section list is the main sidebar's pages; App settings has no presets and is not listed.

**Owner test steps.** (1) Mouse and Trackpad: the Mouse Buttons cogwheel opens Presets at "Mouse and Trackpad", Mouse Buttons outlined; the Trackpad cogwheel moves the same window to Trackpad. (2) "+ Add a layer", type "Laptop", Enter; Switch to; Rename; Delete then Undo. (3) Double-click a layer's shortcut box, press Ctrl+Alt+1; press it anywhere: the layer switches; Shortcuts page > search "Laptop" shows "Switch to layer "Laptop""; give the same key to another preset: refused with the reason. (4) Displays: both cogwheels; "+ Save the current picture as a preset", drag it above another, Apply. (5) Open an FX window with an equaliser, preset box > "Manage presets...": Volume / that equaliser's card; "+ Save the current settings as a preset"; Duplicate a built-in; Export, then Import the file. (6) App settings > Presets > Open. (7) Search "night" across sections. (8) Narrowest window (720 px) in both themes: rows, tags and the shortcut box do not overlap. (9) Quit and relaunch: order, names and shortcuts are kept.

## Mixer rows with knobs, the Sound setup button, clickable setups table (15 Nov 2026; built to `docs/mockups/mixer-rows-knobs.html`) - compiled (Release throwaway build, 0 warnings, 0 errors, folder deleted) and `dotnet test` 520/520 pass (480 before, 1 obsolete hotkey test removed, 41 new in `KnobMathTests`, `MixerRowTests`, `VolumeConstructionTests`); nothing run, seen, clicked or heard; no Windows or audio setting changed; the running app was not stopped

**As built.**
- **Knob** (new master, `Controls/Knob.cs` + `KnobFace.cs` + `KnobMath.cs`, template and colours `Styles/Knobs.axaml`, sizes `Knob*` in App.axaml): a `RangeBase`, so `ValueWheel` (wheel = 1 % of the range, now one rule `Core/Models/Percent.StepOf`) and `SliderReset` (generalised from `Slider` to `RangeBase`) serve it like every slider. 32 px face, 270 degree sweep, grey track (`BrushHoverStrong`, `BrushHoverEdge` on hover), accent value arc (pan from the centre mark, volume from empty), pointer line, accent ring on keyboard focus and while being turned (`.active`). Mouse: drag up/down turns it (`KnobMath.FullTurnPixels` = 160 px for the whole range); a press that moves 3 px or more is a drag; ONE CLICK without a drag opens the value for typing (the owner's explicit rule for knobs; it waits the platform's double-click time first, so a double-click never opens the box); a double-click resets (volume 100, pan centre: `VolumeRow.VolumeReset` / `PanReset`). The typing box is the knob's own `NumberText` (with `OpensOnDoubleClick` off), opened through `Knob.TypeRequestedEvent`, answered once at start-up by `NumberText.Register()`; Enter or clicking away applies, Esc cancels, the keyboard goes back to the knob. Keys: in a card list (the Mixer rows) the knob is a part like a slider: Left/Right walk past it, Enter starts turning it (then Up/Right +1 %, Down/Left -1 %, Shift or PageUp/PageDown 10 %), Enter or Esc end, Home resets, Ctrl+Enter types. Tooltips carry the exact value ("Pan (left to right): L 20", "Volume: 72").
- **This differs from every other control on purpose:** sliders and number texts type on a double-click and reset on a double-click of the slider; a knob types on a single click and resets on a double-click (AGENTS.md principle 4).
- **Mixer row** (`VolumeRowView.axaml`, headings `MixerHeadings.axaml`): icon | name + state word ("playing", "not open", "default") | Plays on chip | Method chip + fact line | pan knob | volume knob | meter | M | FX | x. Name and the two pickers share the spare width 10:11:11 and trim; knobs, meter, M, FX and x are shared Auto columns (`Mixer*`), so the headings and both groups line up. The old sentence ("→ Speakers · 2 effects · status · Switch · Test · Method") is gone.
- **Plays on** (`PlaysOnButton`, `Button.selector`): the master and programs open THE Plays on menu, now one piece for both tabs (`PlaysOnMenu` + `PlaysOnMenuView`, filled by `VolumePageViewModel.FillPlaysOn`, gate `OutputsGateOf`). The Wiring cell keeps its add/remove behaviour (`MarksCurrent` false); the Mixer's menu (`MarksCurrent` true) lists the devices in use ticked, a pick makes that device the only one, and "+ Add a second device" (only where several are allowed) opens Wiring at the row. Same entries, greying, reasons, note and Switch as Wiring. A combined device's chip opens Wiring at its row; a microphone's chip only names its device (`Border.selector`), since Wiring offers no "records from" choice for a microphone.
- **Method chip** (`MethodButton`, `Button.selector.method` + `.apo` green / `.cable` blue (new master brush `BrushCable` / `BrushTagCable` from `Palette.Cable` / `CableInk`) / `.recording` accent / plain = direct): on every row; the method is `SoundModes.ChipMethodOf` (what runs now, else what will run when it plays), its word `ChipWord`. Master and programs open the existing Method menu (`RouteChoice`); in the Fast setup every card is greyed ("setup 8 records every program" / "runs the master in Equalizer APO") and the step under the cards opens the Sound setups table. The program's Test moved into this menu ("Test recording", its result beside it and on the fact line). Microphones and combined devices show the chip as words (no menu exists for them). The fact under the chip is `EngineStatus.Fact` / `SoundModes.FactOf` ("70 ms", "no delay", "on the speakers", "Starts when it plays", "No free cable"...); while a Test result exists it shows that, while Fast cannot record a program it shows "Can't record" with the reason as tooltip; its one link is the Switch, else the row's fix step (Use Recording, Use Equalizer APO, Choose devices). The chip's and fact's tooltip is the row's long line, which now starts with the setup ("Setup 8 · effects ...", `SoundModes.SetupName`), also in the FX window.
- **FX button**: `Button.pill.glow` while `FxSlot.AnyRuns` (effects on and one effect switched on): accent words, edge and wash plus a soft glow (`AccentGlow`, a `BoxShadows` resource made by `AccentService` from the accent, `Palette.Glow*`); a press toggles the row's FX window (`FxWindowService.Toggle`, given to the page as `toggleFx`; the Easy page still uses `Open`).
- **Meters**: kept visible in the row, in their own column beside the volume knob (choice: there is room; 12 px).
- **Sound setup button** (`SoundSetupButton` + `SoundSetupChoice`): "Sound setup (N) v" at the right of the tab row on both tabs; a click opens the Sound setups table directly. The Fast / Full control cards, the panel and the words "Sound mode: ..." are gone from the interface (the modes stay internal; the table's group headings still name them). The table's rows apply their setup on click (as before); the window now has a footer: one toolbar row of the setup steps (Install Equalizer APO when missing, Install VB-CABLE or More cables, Choose devices, Remove VB-CABLE, then OmniControl APO's Advanced install / Preview changes / Remove OmniControl APO) and one status line (the last step's result, else "Applies to every row. Effects run only while OmniControl is open."). The search card "Sound mode" (Fast/Full gallery) became "Sound setup" with a button that opens the table.
- **Hotkey**: "Switch sound mode" is now "Next sound setup" (id `volume.fxmode.next` kept, so hotkeys.json bindings keep working). It applies `SoundModes.NextSetup` = the next setup after the live one that can be chosen (the table's own `CanChoose`) and sets something different (2, 3 and 4 set the same, so on a PC with one cable pair it goes 1 -> 2 -> 5 -> 8 -> 1); greyed on the Shortcuts page with "No other sound setup works on this PC now" when none. Stored settings need no migration: a stored Fast is setup 8; Full control restores its remembered setup (none = setup 1), as before.
- Removed as unused: the Sound mode panel and its cards (`SoundModeCard`), `SoundMode.Description/Details/Tags/Setup/MethodSummary`, `ModeTag`, `ModeSetup`, `SoundModes.Next`, `NextModeUnavailable`, `CardActionWidth`, the row's sentence members (`Lead`, `RouteArrow`, `EffectsText`, `ShowsRouteChoice`...). Renamed: `RouteButton` -> `MethodButton`, `SoundModeButton` -> `SoundSetupButton`, `SoundModeChoice.cs` -> `SoundSetupChoice.cs`, `VolumePageViewModel.SoundMode.cs` -> `.Setups.cs`, resource `WiringHeaderInset` -> `RowHeadingInset`. User-visible "Runs in both sound modes" (FX window) -> "Runs in every sound setup".
- Construction order: written in the remarks of `VolumePageViewModel`'s constructor (setup table and button first, then gates, Wiring groups, search cards, the master row, then the first reads); `VolumeConstructionTests` builds the row, menu and setup pieces in that order with no devices and no programs.

**Differences from the mock-up.** (1) The typing box replaces the value words under the knob rather than lying over the knob face (the face stays visible while typing). (2) A meter column sits between the volume knob and M (the spec allowed it). (3) The "x" column for closed programs and combined devices stays at the far right, as before. (4) The footer's buttons keep the app's existing words ("Install Equalizer APO", "Install VB-CABLE", "More cables") instead of "Get Equalizer APO" / "Get a virtual cable": the same actions are used in Wiring and the Method menu, one wording. (5) The master in the Fast setup without effects says "Direct" / "no delay"; a program passing through the master's cable bus without effects says "Cable bus" / "no effects". (6) Inside the Mixer's card list a knob's arrow keys need Enter first (as a slider does), because Left/Right walk the row's parts there; outside a card list the arrows turn a focused knob directly.

**Open items.** (1) The Switch links still name the internal mode ("Switch to Full control", "Switch to Fast"), as do the setup table's group headings and a few reasons ("Needs Full control"); the spec only removed "Sound mode: ..."; renaming them to setups needs a decision on which setup each Switch should choose. (2) A footer step shows no busy state while it runs; its result appears in the status line. (3) The FX button does not show whether its window is open (only lit by running effects). (4) At the narrowest window (680 px) the name and the two chips get about 50 px each and trim hard; headings trim too. (5) The chips are reached by the keyboard, their menus are mouse first (AGENTS.md rule 5), like the Wiring menu.

**Unverified (nothing could be seen).** The knob drawing (arc angles, ring, centre mark) in both themes; the glow's look; the double-click timing; the typing box inside the knob; the headings' alignment with the rows; the narrowest window; the search results view of a Mixer row (its shared columns are outside a size scope there, so each sizes itself).

**Owner test steps.** (1) Volume > Advanced > Mixer: each row shows Plays on, Method (coloured), the fact under it, two knobs, meter, M, FX; headings above each group. (2) Drag a volume knob up and down; wheel over it; double-click it (back to 100); single-click it, type 40, Enter. Pan: drag, double-click (C). (3) Keyboard: Up/Down to a row, Left/Right to the volume knob, Enter, Up/Down/Shift+Up, Enter; Home resets. (4) Plays on of a program: pick another device (ticked now), "Master (default device)"; in Full control "+ Add a second device" opens Wiring at that row; Wiring's cell menu still adds and removes as before. (5) Method of a program in Full control: pick Recording, Cable; in setup 8 every card is dimmed with the reason and "Sound setups…" opens the table; Test recording while the program plays. (6) FX: with an effect on the button glows; press: the FX window opens; press again: it closes. (7) "Sound setup (N) v": the table opens; click an available row: the badge changes; greyed rows say why; footer steps work and their result shows in the status line. (8) Shortcuts: "Next sound setup" keeps an old binding and steps through the working setups. (9) Both themes, narrowest window.

### Mixer row tweaks (meter last, arrow cursor, chip line)
- Row order is now icon | name | Plays on | Method | Pan | Volume | M | FX | x | meter (`VolumeRowView`, `MixerHeadings`; `MixerMeter` is the last shared column). Rows are two grid rows: both chips top-aligned on row 1, the fact line under the Method chip on row 2; all other cells span both rows and centre.
- Knob cursor is the plain arrow (`Styles/Knobs.axaml`), no size cursor.
- `LevelMeter` draws a 1 px rounded `BrushHoverEdge` frame so it stays visible on a hovered (grey) row. Unverified on screen: spacing, row height with an empty fact line, frame contrast.

## Effects switch remembers "the user turned it off" (FxUserOff)

`FxUserOff` (`MasterFxUserOff` on `MixerSettings`; `FxUserOff` on `AppAudioEntry`, `InputFxEntry`, `CombinedDevice`; old files load false) is set only by `MixerService.SetFxByUser(key, false)`: the FX window's Effects switch, the Volume rows' FX toggles and the master hotkey. `SetFxOn(..., true)` (Easy setups, anything automatic) clears it; restore defaults clears it (`AudioDefaults`, undo puts it back). `MixerService.EffectsAdded(key)` is called when a chain grows in the FX window (Add effect, paste); it switches the effects on unless `FxUserOff`. Removing effects never touches the flag. Open: presets that add effects were not found as a separate path; if one exists outside `FxChainViewModel` it must call `EffectsAdded`. Tests: `FxUserOffTests` (rule, old file, restore); the `MixerService` setters themselves need Windows and are not tested.

## Display rotation and suggested keys

- Each display in Displays > Hardware (the built-in one too) has an Orientation gallery card (Landscape, Portrait, Landscape (flipped), Portrait (flipped)), applied at once through `ChangeDisplaySettingsEx` with `CDS_UPDATEREGISTRY` (so Windows keeps it). The card is read from Windows on refresh; if Windows will not say, it is greyed with a reason. A refusal shows one sentence in the status line and the detail goes to `display-log.txt`. `GalleryItem` gained `Availability`, `Hint` and `Load`. The picture shows all four orientations as small clickable screens (current one in the accent), so the user picks before the display turns (owner asked: a wrong turn is hard to read back).
- Four hotkeys "Rotate to ..." (Displays - Hardware - Orientation) with the all/some-monitors chooser. Mapping used: Portrait = DMDO_270, Portrait (flipped) = DMDO_90 (what Windows Settings calls them); not verified on a real screen.
- `HotkeyAction.SuggestedKey` is optional and never bound by itself. "Use suggested keys" on a Shortcuts panel header binds the keys of actions that have none yet and skips (and names) any whose key another action holds. Rotation suggestions: Ctrl+Alt+Up Landscape, Right Portrait, Down Landscape (flipped), Left Portrait (flipped). Any future action gets the pill by setting `SuggestedKey`.
- Unverified: nothing was run (no rotation on the real screen). Build into a throwaway folder succeeded. Open: the pill is on the panel header only (not on subgroup headers); the card is not in the hardware Restore defaults (rotation is not reset by it); a rotation changes other displays' positions, which the page learns of at its next rescan.


## Sound setups table removed; the Sound setup button is now an option-card pop-down (15 Nov 2026) - compiled (Release throwaway build, 0 warnings, 0 errors, folder deleted); nothing run or seen

Owner: "remove the Sound setups table window entirely". Done:

- **Gone:** `SetupTable.cs`, `SetupTableWindow.axaml(.cs)`, `Button.tablerow` and `Border.tablegroup` in `Styles/Tables.axaml` (the file now holds only `Border.badge` and `TextBlock.cautiontext`), the `Table*` / `SetupTable*` sizes in `App.axaml`, the "Sound setups..." search card (the page's search words still find the button), `HelpGroup` and `HelpCombination.Group` (no group headings), the Method menu's Fast-setup step "Sound setups..." (the cards there stay greyed with their reasons).
- **New:** the Sound setup button opens a pop-down in the Method menu's design (`FlyoutPresenter.optionmenu`, `Button.optioncard`): one `SetupCard` per setup from `SoundModes.HelpCombinations` (name "Setup N", one muted line: what the master runs on and what the cable is for, the live one `.selected`, a setup that can't work greyed with its one-line reason and as tooltip; Fast's setup 8 stays choosable). A click applies it (`VolumePageViewModel.ChooseSetup`) and closes the pop-down. The footer steps (Install Equalizer APO, VB-CABLE, Choose devices, Remove, advanced install) sit under a `Border.hrule` as `Button.pill.cardaction`, their result in a muted line below. Keys through the shared `OptionPanelKeys` (Up/Down between cards, Esc closes). The pop-down reads the PC again each time it opens (`SoundSetupChoice.Refresh`).
- Fast mode still exists (setup 8): kept as the last card, no group headings. Switching a mode from a gate's Switch link is unchanged.
- **Dropped with the table:** the columns "Programs with effects" and "Master effects" and the "Applies to every row. Effects run only while OmniControl is open." line (the Method menu and rows already say these things). Open item: if the owner wants them back they belong in the cards' tooltips.
- **Unverified:** nothing seen on screen: the pop-down's width and height with eight cards plus steps (it may need a scroll at small window heights), the reason line's look on a greyed card, both themes. `dotnet test` could not run: `tests/OmniControl.Tests/ShortcutGroupingTests.cs(38)` fails to compile for a reason that predates this change (`HotkeyGroup` needs `useSuggested`); the tests I changed (`SetupTests`, `MixerRowTests`, `AvailabilityTests`, `VolumeConstructionTests`) compile.

## Sound setups removed; Volume presets added (15 Nov 2026) - compiled (throwaway build, folder deleted) and `dotnet test` 505/505 pass; nothing run or seen; no setting of the owner's changed; the running app was not stopped

Owner: remove the whole "Sound setup" concept (the user sets every row's Method and the Wiring by hand) and add Volume presets in the program's preset design. This supersedes the setup sections above (setups table, Sound setup button, "Next sound setup" hotkey, setup memory).

- **Gone:** `SoundSetupButton.axaml(.cs)`, `SoundSetupChoice.cs`, `SetupCard.cs`, `Audio/SoundModes.Help.cs` (`HelpCombinations`, `HelpFacts`, `HelpWorks`, `SetupChoice`, `WorksOf`, `SetupNumber`, `SetupOf`, `RestoreSetup`, `SetupName`, `SetupLine`, `NextSetup`...; `Capitalised` / `Lowered` moved to `SoundModes.Availability.cs`), `MixerService.Setups.cs` (`ApplySetup`, setup memory), `MixerSettings.FullSetup` and `FreedCables` (old files load without them; cables that setup 5 had switched off stay off and can be switched on in Wiring > Virtual cables), `RoutePlan.Help` / `EffectsEngine.Help`, the "Next sound setup" hotkey (`volume.fxmode.next`: a key bound to it is no longer used), `ChooseSetup`, the badge and `Border.badge` with its `Badge*` sizes, `SetupTests.cs` and the setup tests in `AvailabilityTests`, `MixerRowTests`, `VolumeConstructionTests`.
- **Lines:** a row's long line and the FX window's engine line no longer start with "Setup N · " (`EngineStatus.LineFor(method)` / `TipFor(method)`).
- **Fast / Full control stay** (the engine and every gate are built on them). Switching: a gate's Switch link (unchanged), and in Fast mode every Method menu (all cards greyed, "Fast mode records every program; switch to Full control to choose") shows the step "Switch to Full control" (`FullControlStep`; refused with one line when Full control can't run here). Back to Fast: the gates' "Switch to Fast" links and Easy mode, as before. Open item: there is no plain "Switch to Fast" step in the Method menu; say if one is wanted.
- **Install steps moved** to the foot of the master's Method menu, under a `Border.hrule`, as `Button.pill.cardaction` (the same look the setup pop-down's foot had): `RouteChoice.Steps` / `SyncSteps` (the fix step's own label is not repeated), filled from `SetupSteps()` (Install Equalizer APO, Install VB-CABLE or More cables, Choose devices, Remove VB-CABLE, Advanced install / Preview changes / Remove OmniControl APO). The per-row fix steps (`MasterApoFix`, `MicApoFix`, `ProgramMethodFix`), the Wiring tab's Install VB-CABLE pill and Virtual cables "Get more cables" are unchanged. A step's result now shows only in the main window's status line (the pop-down's note line is gone). `.Setups.cs` became `.Steps.cs`; `CardAction` has its own file.
- **Volume presets:** a `HeaderPicker` (drop-down + cogwheel, as Mouse Buttons / Trackpad / Displays) where the Sound setup button was, at the right of the tab row on both tabs. `Audio/VolumePreset.cs` (`VolumePreset`: name, id, `Setup` = a `MixerSettings` snapshot, null = empty; `VolumePresetFile` = `volume-presets.json`, three empty "Preset 1".."Preset 3" by default, `ActiveId`), `Audio/VolumePresets.cs` (made by the shell, disposed before the mixer): like the mouse layers one preset is always in force and every mixer change (`Updated`, `FxChanged`, `CablesChanged`) is saved into it when the setup differs (`AudioDefaults.SameSetup`); choosing another first keeps the live setup in the one being left, then applies the chosen one through `AudioDefaults.Apply` (the restore's own single step); an empty preset applies the audio defaults (so switching to an empty preset clears effects, combined devices and methods; the preset left keeps them). What a preset holds = exactly what the restore of audio defaults covers: every chain and its switch (master, programs, microphones, combined devices), each program's Method and Plays on and "added" mark, the master's method and devices, combined devices, cable switches and marks, the sound mode. **Not** volumes, pan and mutes (they would be rewritten on every knob turn; say if they should be in). Programs that are not remembered at apply time are skipped (as the undo does). The group `volume.presets` ("Volume presets", section Volume) registers through `PresetGroups` (`VolumePresetProvider`: the one in force "in use" and not deletable, an empty one tagged "empty"; rename, move, add, shortcut, Switch to come from the shared manager; shortcuts appear in Shortcuts through `PresetShortcuts`). No shared mechanism had to be extended.
- **Unverified:** nothing seen on screen (the master Method menu's height with the steps, the picker at the narrowest window beside the Wiring cable chip and pill, both themes); applying a preset on real devices (how the engine re-plans when mode, method and combined devices change in one step); no unit test for `VolumePresets` (it needs the real `MixerService` and writes to app data).

## Automation page (rules, blocks, custom actions)

Built: sidebar page "Automation" with tabs Rules and Custom actions (`Pages/Automation/`, logic in `App/Automation/`, see ARCHITECTURE.md).
- Rules: "When [trigger] [and condition] do [action] [then action]" rows with a switch, a name, "Ran ..." / "Next: ..." tags and a delete cross; "+ Add automation". Triggers: at log in, OmniControl starts, time of day and days (optionally wakes the PC), after a timer (once or repeating), idle for N min, wakes from sleep, starts running on battery, plugged in, a program starts or closes, a USB device connects or disconnects, I press a hotkey. Conditions: on battery, plugged in, between two times, on days, a program is running. Every block opens the shared picker; the action picker is the mouse buttons' list (the live master list) without the raw "run a command" step (use a custom action) and without other rules' "Run:" actions (no rule starts a rule).
- Hotkey: not stored on the rule. Each Do rule registers "Run: name" in the master list; the hotkey block edits that action's shortcut (same capture and "one key, one action" behaviour as the Shortcuts page, plus `ReservedKeys`). The action also appears in Shortcuts, the mouse and gesture pickers and search, and runs the rule when the rule is on and its conditions hold. Renaming a rule makes its action again (the shortcut is kept).
- Blocks: "While [condition] don't allow [action]": enforced at the one gate in `HotkeyRegistry` (`Permitted`, used by hotkeys, `Run(id)` from mouse buttons, rules, and `RunLevel`). Only master actions can be blocked.
- Custom actions: name, type (PowerShell command, script file .ps1/.bat/.cmd, program, file or folder, http/https address), target, arguments; each registers once as "name" under Automation - Custom actions. Custom actions are their own section ("Custom actions", after Launch) of the Choose-the-action window. "+ New custom action..." is a master entry (`AutomationService.MakeNewCustomAction`, a picker command with an `ActionEditor.Create`): selecting it shows the new-action form in the settings pane (name, type, target with Browse, arguments); "Create custom action" registers it, lists it selected and it can be used with "Use this action". Each custom action shows the same fields live and a "Remove custom action" button (no confirm there; the tab still confirms). The tab and the picker edit the same data through `CustomFields` / `AutomationService.EditCustom`. The form entry now shows in every action picker (mouse, gestures), not only rule steps. Open: the Target's Browse for "Open file or folder" and "Run script file" picks a file only (a folder is typed); not run in the app.
- New built-in actions (Automation - Power / Control): Sleep, Hibernate, Shut down, Restart, Sign out (countdown), Lock, Power mode (Best efficiency, Balanced, Best performance), Open OmniControl, Pause all rules.
- Safety: never-blockable flag (Lock, Open OmniControl, Pause all rules) and `ReservedKeys` (emergency key and Windows' own keys); Ctrl+Alt+Shift+P and the tray item pause all rules and blocks (a block never applies while paused); Shift held at start = all rules off for the session (status line says so); minimum gap between starts and a burst pause with one plain line (`Timing.Automation*`); Sleep/Hibernate/Shut down/Restart/Sign out wait `Timing.PowerActionGrace` after start and always show a cancellable `Timing.PowerCountdown` (hotkeys and mouse included, not only rules); custom actions run as the normal user, hidden, stopped after `Timing.CustomActionTimeout`, failures are one sentence with the log `automation-log.txt`; PowerShell runs with `-ExecutionPolicy Bypass` for that one process only (owner's choice, 16 Nov 2026; the machine policy is unchanged); a program step in a rule is confirmed once (hash kept in `automation.json`).
- Search words: schedule, timer, task, scheduler, automation, wake, power.

Open items / choices to overrule:
- "At log in" fires when Windows starts OmniControl (the `--background` start-up entry), not on every sign-in or unlock; the row says "Needs Start with Windows" when that is off.
- "Wakes from sleep" is seen as a jump of the clock between two looks (no power broadcast); the wake timer only wakes a sleeping or hibernating PC, never one that is off, and Windows must allow wake timers.
- The built-in mouse button actions (`desktop.lock`, window actions, keystrokes) do not pass the gate; only master actions can be blocked. The old "Lock workstation" (Win+L keystroke) now duplicates the new Lock action; it was kept so stored mappings still work (a candidate for removal with a migration).
- Rule sentences have no Left/Right walking between blocks (Tab, Enter, Delete work); the gesture rule editor has it. A shared walker in `SentenceFocus` is the next step. No "Restore defaults" for this page yet.
- The sentence hotkey block is a mouse-first control (click, then press keys); reachable by Tab and Enter.
- Not checked: nothing was run; shutdown, sleep, scripts and wake timers were never executed. `PowerSetActiveOverlayScheme` and the overlay GUIDs are from memory of Windows' values and are unverified on this PC; the page was not looked at in the narrowest window or both themes (it reuses the master block, tag, pill and group styles only). Build clean and `AutomationTests` pass (516 tests in all).

## IR Loader: TabForge's settings and the sound in OmniControl's engine

TabForge's IR Loader (read-only clone, 3774ab6) has Mix, Gain, Auto level, Trim silence, Separate Left/Right files and an A/B bypass. Added to OmniControl's IR Loader: **Auto level** (switch, default on: the loudest frequency of the file at 0 dB) and **Trim silence** (switch, default off: cuts the lead-in below -60 dB and the tail below -90 dB), appended after Mix and Gain (`IrParams`), so old chains load (padded with those defaults). The IR Loader now also runs in **OmniControl's own engine** (`FxEngines.Program | Apo`; before it was Equalizer APO only, which is why rows on cables, microphones and combined devices said "saved only, not run here: IR Loader"): `IrStage` convolves with zero added latency, then Gain, then the effect's own Mix; the file is prepared on a worker and the sound passes unchanged until it is ready or if the file is missing or unreadable (nothing says so on screen yet: open item). Only WAV files run in OmniControl's engine (.flac/.aif run only in Equalizer APO).
- **Equalizer APO:** its `Convolution:` command takes only a file, so the file, Gain and Auto level apply (Auto level is written as a Preamp worked out from the WAV; a non-WAV file gets none). Trim silence does not apply there, nor does a Mix below 100 % (as before). The effect's tooltip on the FX window says so (`RunsDetail`); the engine status line is unchanged.
- **Not added:** Separate Left/Right files (needs two more file paths in `FxSlot`) and A/B bypass (the effect's own on/off switch already does that). The IR's Mix is the effect's own parameter, separate from the slot's Wet mix, as before.
- **Unverified:** `dotnet test` is blocked by Windows App Control (0x800711C7); `tests/OmniControl.Tests/IrLoaderTests.cs` (convolver against direct convolution, old-chain padding) compiles but has not run. No listening test; Equalizer APO's own level handling of Convolution (whether it normalises) was not checked, so Auto level in Equalizer APO may double up if it does.

## Hotkey box click and preview, Automation joins and waits, "Turn rules on" (15 Nov 2026) - compiled (throwaway build, deleted), `dotnet test` 521/521 pass (after one `-p:Deterministic=false` rebuild); nothing run or seen, app not stopped

- **Hotkey box** (`Hotkeys/HotkeyBox`, `HotkeyCapture`): one click (or Enter) listens, the box pulses in the accent (the existing `TextBox.listening` style) and says "Press keys...". Held modifiers show live ("Ctrl + Alt + ..."), the main key shows the whole combination, and the shortcut is saved when the main key is released (`HotkeyCapture.TryHandleUp`, wired in the box and in the Automation row). Esc cancels, Backspace/Delete clears, plain letters are still refused, `ReservedKeys` and the conflict checks are unchanged (they act on `Gesture`). Idle watermark "Click to set". The Automation trigger block uses the same `HotkeyCapture` but is a button, not the box: it shows "Press keys..." only (no live preview, no pulse, because the sentence is rebuilt on each change and would drop the keyboard).
- **Automation joins** (rules saved before keep working: missing joins read "and", missing waits "then"): triggers get `AutomationRule.MoreTriggers` (joined by "or", shown as plain "or" words, "+ or" adds one; never a hotkey; each keeps its own timer/idle state in `AutomationService.Triggers`); conditions have `ConditionJoins` ("and" / "or" / "and not", left to right, `AutomationJoins.Holds`, used by Do rules and Block rules); a Do rule's actions have `StepWaits` ("then" / "then wait N s", N 1..`Timing.AutomationMaxWaitSeconds` = 600, starts at 5). The join words are `Button.blockjoin` with a `listmenu` pop-down (`SentenceLink`); the wait has a slider plus typed value in the same pop-down (wheel, double-click reset, typing). Choosing "then wait" closes the pop-down; click the word again to set the seconds. `RuleRunner.RunAsync` takes the waits and a stop token; pausing all rules (or closing) stops running rules between steps and in a wait (logged, not shown as a failure). The gesture rule editor's own and/or flip is unchanged (it still has two states).
- **Wait wording:** after a "then wait N s" join the sentence adds the plain word ", then do" (`AutomationItem.AddActions`, a `SentenceWord`) before the next action: "then wait 70 s, then do <action>". Plain "then" joins are unchanged.
- **Turn rules on:** `AutomationService.TurnRulesOn()` ends the Shift safe-start state at once (starts the look and watchers; the "When OmniControl starts" and "At log in" triggers are not replayed). Status line and report: "Rules are off: Shift was held when OmniControl started." with a `Button.link` "Turn rules on" on the Automation page.
- Open: the Block rules' actions still join with a plain "and" (a set, nothing to pick).


## Automation: hotkey trigger capture and Custom actions row (15 Nov 2026)

- Choosing "I press a hotkey" (or clicking the rule's "I press" block) listens through the rule's own action and opens a 250 px capture pop-down under the block (`Popup` in `AutomationRuleRow.axaml`, `FlyoutPresenter.capturemenu`): live keycaps (`Hotkeys/KeycapRow`, `Border.keycap`), dashed accent slot, pulsing ring (`Border.capturebox`), "Press the keys now" (or "Add Ctrl or Alt" after a refused plain key), Clear and Cancel (`Button.pill.cardaction`, not focusable so keys stay with the row). Keys, Esc, Delete, save-on-release and the conflict check are still `HotkeyCapture` and the action; no second implementation. The block is a `SentenceHotkey` ("I press" + keycaps, or "I press" + dashed "set keys" with the rule tag "Needs a hotkey").
- The pop-down is placed on the block when the rebuilt block loads (`OnHotkeyBlockLoaded`) and closes when the rule stops listening; the sentence is rebuilt on every listening change, so there is no flyout on the block itself.
- Differences from `docs/mockups/hotkey-trigger-capture.html`: the pop-down ring pulses its own border (as `TextBox.listening` does) instead of a glow; the block has a small remove cross like every other block; sizes and colours come from the theme (keycap fill `BrushHover`, edge `BrushHoverEdge`), so they differ slightly from the mock-up's literals; the pop-down opens under the block's left edge with the master 2 px offset, not an 76 px margin.
- Open: the Shortcuts `HotkeyBox` and the preset manager still show plain text; they can adopt `KeycapRow`. Not run in the app (rule 7); the pop-down placement after the sentence rebuilds needs the owner's eye.
- Custom actions row: the type drop-down used `ComboBox.choice`, which spans two grid columns and drew under Run; it now uses `choice half` (one column), and the name box has a 120 px minimum so the row cannot collapse.

- Automation: "+ New automation / block / custom action" are `Button.pill`s on each group's title line; an empty list shows `Pages/EmptyListRow` (dashed row, sentence, link). Mock-up: `docs/mockups/automation-new-buttons.html`. Differences: none known; the title-line grid is local to `AutomationPage.axaml` (not a shared header piece, none fits a plain title). Not run in the app.

## Choose-the-action window in three panes (15 Nov 2026) - compiled (throwaway build, deleted) and `dotnet test` run (540 passed); nothing was driven on the PC (rule 7)

Mock-up: `docs/mockups/action-picker-three-pane.html`. Sections | entries | settings of the highlighted entry (always shown, live, no Back), one search over every section (a match shows its section as a small muted label), footer = only "Use this action". Sections are the sidebar pages in the sidebar's order with their icons, read from the live page list (`ShellViewModel` -> `HotkeyRegistry.UseSections`), then Keys and windows, Power, Launch, Recent. A page with no action is simply not listed. Entries place themselves: `HotkeyAction.PickerSection` / `Group` / `Subgroup` (-> `PickerHeading`), built-ins by `Home` in `MouseActionCatalog`; there is no side list.

**Differences from the mock-up:** (1) an extra section **Keys and windows** holds the built-in keystroke, window, desktop, media and browser actions, which belong to no page (the owner's list named only pages, Power, Launch, Recent); (2) the highlight of an entry that starts a group also covers its small heading (the heading is part of the row); (3) no "Warn on screen" setting and no "Never runs within 2 min of start-up" line: the countdown is the on-screen warning (5 to 300 s, never none) and the 2-minute grace stays in `PowerGuard`; (4) Recent keeps the last 8 used entries (`recent-actions.json`); (5) window 880 x 560, minimum 720 x 400; text and path settings sit under their label so a narrow settings pane holds them; (6) a click on an entry only highlights it, a double-click, Enter or "Use this action" uses it (the settings are beside it, so the old one-click pick is gone).

## Master preset picker (15 Nov 2026)

`Controls/PresetPicker` (replaces `Pages/HeaderPicker`; mock-up `docs/mockups/preset-picker-manage-in-list.html`): a drop-down of a group's presets whose list ends with a thin line (`Border.hrule`) and a "Manage presets…" row (`ManageEntry`). Choosing it raises `ManageClicked` (the page opens the manager at its group, as the cog did) and puts the selection back; choosing a preset applies it live through the `ChoiceItem`. The separate cogwheel is gone. The wheel and Left/Right never land on the Manage row (`ComboKeys.Step`); with the list open it is the last item for Up/Down/End.
Places converted (audit): Mouse Buttons (mouse layers), Trackpad (trackpad layers), Displays Hardware (monitor presets), Displays Software (display presets), Volume (tab row, both tabs). Not presets, left as they are: the `Button.pill.cog` on action chips and gesture rows (action settings), the Easy "Sound ▾" buttons (a menu of presets plus sliders), and the FX window's per-effect preset ComboBox (no manager entry; open item: give it the picker if its kind's preset group should be manageable from there).

## App lists and the Apps scope (15 Nov 2026) - compiled (throwaway build, deleted), `dotnet test` 552/552 pass (4 new in `AppScopeTests`, `RestoreDefaultsTests` property count 8 to 10); nothing run or seen, no setting changed, the running app was not stopped

Mock-up: `docs/mockups/app-scope-and-lists.html`. Master store `App/AppLists/AppListStore` (app-lists.json, empty by default); the scope is `AppScopeSetting` (All / Only / Except + list id, null = not limited) behind one model (`AppScopeModel`, made by `AppScopes`), one chip (`Controls/AppScopeChip`) and one menu (`Controls/AppScopeMenuView`). An app is its `ProgramIdentity` id ("chrome": exe name, lower case) plus a display name. Used on the Mouse page's Mouse Buttons group header (left of the layer picker) and, because that group lives on the Gestures page, on "Scroll over a screen area". The "N settings" count is gone on those two headers (`Summary` empty). Enforcement: `MouseService.Handle` treats a button outside its scope as unmapped (it passes through; log line "not for this app"); `GestureService.Handle` lets the scroll through. Both ask `AppScopeGate` (foreground program via `Core/Engine/ProgramIdentity`, only looked up while some scope limits).
- Storage: `MouseSettings.ButtonsScope` / `CardScopes` (by button name), `GestureSettings.AreasScope` / `GestureRule.Scope`. Both are in the restore-defaults copies (a restore sets every app). Scope is NOT part of the mouse layer presets (a preset is one layer's mappings; the scope is the group's, whatever layer is in force). Trackpad gestures, middle-click areas and hold-a-key scrolls have no scope (not asked for).
- Deleting a list: the manager asks once when it is in use, then `AppListStore.Delete` resets every registered user to All apps (group: null, card: an explicit All).
- Differences from the mock-up: (1) the "current" row in the Works in menu is ticked (as the Plays on menu) instead of outlined in the accent; (2) "Only in..." / "All except..." open a second page inside the same pop-down (a "‹ Works in" row goes back) instead of a side submenu; (3) a card's cogwheel now always shows on the Mouse Buttons and screen-area cards and opens the Works in menu, with "Action settings..." as its first row when the action has settings (a cog click no longer opens the action window directly there); (4) the card's tag sits in the card's lower line (with the Windows tags), not beside the name, so the card keeps its width; (5) the manager's Rename edits in place at the top, Delete is a pill, no separate "Done".
- "+ Installed app" lists the catalogue's installed programs (`AppActions.InstalledPrograms`; Store apps have no exe and are left out; Discord and Teams are named through their updater's `--processStart` argument). "+ Running app" lists programs with a window now (`ProgramIdentity.Running`).
- Open items: (a) the foreground check names the program of the active window only; an app that shows its windows from a different process than its main exe (some launchers) needs that other exe added; (b) other features (hotkeys, rules, OSD) are not scoped yet: each is one `AppScopes.Group` + one `AppScopeGate.Allows` call; (c) unverified (rule 7): the chip and menu at the narrowest window in both themes, the cog flyout focus return, the manager's pop-downs, the Gestures group header at narrow width.



### Monitor presets and the greyed preset picker

- The master `Controls/PresetPicker` shows only the current value while unavailable (reason in the tooltip); the Displays `PresetPicker` gives it "No presets". Not run on the real laptop.
