## Devices page, Scripts method (16 Nov 2026) - compiled (throwaway builds, deleted); nothing run or seen, app not stopped
Mock-up: `docs/mockups/devices-tab.html`. New sidebar page "Devices" (after Automation): devices list (`ListBox.nav`) + "+ Add device", the chosen device on the right (name, Method chip, state tag, On, Off, delete; Address, Sign-in, Actions rows; set-up checklist until the plug passed its Test; one thin status line with the last command). Code map in `ARCHITECTURE.md`.
- Only the Scripts method exists; Simulation is not built or shown. `DeviceMethods` is the master list for later.
- kasa: `kasa.exe` in the Python's Scripts folder (or the user's `%APPDATA%\Python\*\Scripts`); if absent but `import kasa` works, `python -m kasa`. Arguments `--host <ip> --device-family SMART.TAPOPLUG --encrypt-type KLAP --login-version 2 on|off`. Account goes only in `KASA_USERNAME` / `KASA_PASSWORD` environment variables of that one process (so never in arguments or logs). Install pill: `python -m pip install python-kasa` (the found Python, or `py` launcher), normal user, output in `devices-log.txt`.
- Password: Windows Credential Manager, target `OmniControl/device/<id>` (Core `WindowsCredentialStore`); removed with the device. `devices.json` and backups hold name, address, email, method, plus two flags the mock-up did not list: `Reserved` (the user's confirmation that the address is reserved in the router) and `Verified` (the Test worked once; hides the checklist). Restoring a backup brings the devices back but each needs its password typed again.
- Actions "<name>: On / Off / Toggle" (category Devices, group Plugs) registered per device by id (a rename keeps shortcuts). Toggle follows the last state OmniControl set (unknown counts as off, so the first Toggle turns on); a switch made by hand is not seen. Rate limit: `LaunchLimiter` (3 per 5 s) plus one command at a time per plug.
- Differences from the mock-up: the Method chip is words only (`Border.selector.method`), not a menu, because it has one entry; checklist states are `Border.tag`s (done / next / later) instead of glyphs; the Router guide is an `optionmenu` pop-down with four steps and a link that opens `http://<default gateway>`; the Test pill (off, then on) is the checklist's last step. Extra: a "Get Python" pill when no Python is found, and the Reserved switch beside the address.
- Skipped / unverified: nothing was run against a real plug or router; the Credential Manager calls, the kasa command line flags for the installed python-kasa version, the pop-down and the layouts at the narrowest width and in both themes were not seen. `dotnet test` could not run here (Windows Application Control blocked the freshly built `OmniControl.Core.dll`); the new `DevicesTests` compile. `DeviceService` is not unit tested because `HotkeyRegistry` is not constructible without the app. Kasa's output is only used for the log.
- Open: no Devices entries in global search (only the page keywords and its actions); Devices is not in "Restore defaults".

## Devices page, Simulation method (16 Nov 2026) - compiled (Release throwaway build, 0 errors, the one warning is not from this job, folder deleted); tests compile but could not run here (Windows Application Control blocks the fresh `OmniControl.Core.dll`, as before); nothing run or seen, adb never run, the app not stopped
Mock-up: `docs/mockups/devices-simulation.html` (extends `devices-tab.html`). Code map in `ARCHITECTURE.md` (Devices row). Method is chosen per device in the header chip (`Controls/ChipMenu` + new `Controls/MenuRowList`): Scripts / Simulation; the old "Only Scripts exists" note above no longer holds.
- Set-up checklist (same rows and tags as Scripts): emulator found (Get it), adb found (Get adb), adb connected (Connect), the app set up inside the emulator (Open emulator; done once a recording has an Open app step), steps recorded, tested. Above it the Emulator row (chip menu: installed ones to pick, missing ones tagged "Get it" = their OFFICIAL page in the browser, nothing downloaded) and the ADB address row (auto from the emulator's default, editable). Android Studio's "Open emulator" starts its first virtual device; BlueStacks/LDPlayer start their program.
- Steps: Open app, Wait for, Tap (shown "Wait for [x] - tap it"), Type text, Swipe (up/down/left/right from the screen's middle), Press Back, Check. Elements are found by text, description, resource id (text and description must match whole, ignoring case; the id narrows); position only when a step has none of those. Each waits `Timing.DeviceSimElementWait`, looks every `DeviceSimPoll`, settles `DeviceSimSettle`; a run stops after `DeviceSimRunTimeout`. First failure stops the run, outlines that row red with the reason, and the status line says "step N: ...". Edit = click a block (text boxes live), remove = the cross or Delete, reorder = drag the row (`ListDragReorder`), "+ add step" (a new step goes before the closing Check), Re-pick, Test step, Record, Test all.
- On / Off / Toggle: when the recording ends with a Check and has a Tap before it, that last Tap is the switch: the screen holding it is read, On/Off do nothing ("already on, nothing tapped") when the Check text already shows (or not) as wanted, Toggle always taps; after the tap the Check must show (or stop showing) its text. Without that shape every step just runs in order. Toggle on this method follows the screen, not the last command.
- Recording approach used: tap capture over adb, not the Re-pick-only fallback. `adb shell getevent -l` is read for touches (`AdbTouchWatcher`/`TouchReader`, scaled from the touch device's range to `wm size`), and the screen is dumped (`uiautomator`) every `DeviceSimRecordGap`; a tap becomes a Tap step on the element under it from the dump taken just before, a swipe a Swipe step. Re-pick uses the same watcher for one touch (for a Check, the words under the finger become its text). Record asks first when steps exist, starts with an Open app step for the app in the foreground, and on Stop adds an empty Check that Re-pick fills ("tap the words that show while it is on"). While recording or picking the device counts as busy.
- Limits and unverified: nothing was run against adb or any emulator. getevent may not report touches on every emulator (then Record says so; add steps by hand with "+ add step" and edit them); screen rotation is not handled; a dump taken a moment late can match a fast second tap to the old screen; `input text` only types plain ASCII; the BlueStacks/LDPlayer/Android Studio default addresses, program paths and "turn adb on" notes (`Emulators.All`) come from memory, not from an installed copy; ADB is only looked up in the Android SDK folders and the PATH (not the emulators' own bundled adb, whose server version can clash). The pop-down editor's data context in `Button.Flyout`, the layouts at the narrowest width and in both themes were not seen. The checklist's "Open emulator" and the Emulator row's pill do the same thing; kept because the checklist is where the mock-up puts it.
- Differences from the mock-up: step rows show a step's note (failure reason, or what it still needs) in the middle and Re-pick / Test as links at the right, the remove cross is inside the block (`Button.blockx`), Back and Swipe have a block too ("Press [Back]"); "Last run" is the existing status line ("Last command 08:02 - ..."); the checklist states are tags, as on Scripts. Per-step timeout is not editable.
- New shared pieces: `Controls/MenuRowList` (+ `MenuRowItem`), `Border.steprow` / `.failed`, Core `Emulators` and `SimSteps` master lists. `DeviceMethods.Tip` is the one place for a method's sentence.
- Open: Devices entries in global search and "Restore defaults" (as for Scripts); Simulation devices have no Address/Sign-in rows (their password lives in the emulator); a device switched from Scripts to Simulation keeps both settings.

- Steps: right-click menu (Copy, Cut, Paste below, Duplicate, Delete) = shared `ListMenuFlyout` + `MenuRowList` opened from `ContextRequested` (documented in AGENTS.md master list); clipboard is per device view-model (no paste across devices). ## Emulator keep-running, MuMu and MEmu (16 Nov 2026) - compiled only; adb and the emulators were never run, nothing seen

Built to `docs/mockups/emulator-keep-running.html`. `Devices/EmulatorKeeper.cs` wakes a stopped emulator before an action (`DeviceService.ReachAsync(wake: true)`; the recorder never wakes it), waits for `sys.boot_completed`, and closes it with `adb emu kill` (else `reboot -p`) per the chip. Settings (`EmulatorKeepSettings`, in devices.json) are one set for the PC; idle default `Timing.EmulatorIdleMinutes` 30. Quick boot only after one good cold boot (`ColdBootWorked`); a quick boot that does not finish in `Timing.EmulatorQuickBootTimeout` is killed and one cold boot is tried. "Start with Windows" turns on OmniControl's own start-up entry (`StartupRegistration`) and wakes the first simulated device's emulator at start.
Differences from the mock-up: the old Open emulator pill became Show (opens it, or restarts it with a window when it runs hidden); the idle minutes are typed in a small `NumberText` row inside the menu (double-click), not in the row's words; the menu closes after a switch is clicked; the state tag is two tags (green running, plain stopped); the memory figure refreshes with the page, not on a timer.
Open items: hidden start exists only for Android Studio (`EmulatorInfo.CanHide`), the others always show their window; Close now for non-Studio emulators relies on `reboot -p` and is unverified. MuMu Player 12 and MEmu (install paths, process names, default adb addresses 127.0.0.1:16384 and 127.0.0.1:21503, how to open them) are UNVERIFIED: not installed here.

## Emulator list numbers (Core `Emulators.All`)

UsesGb = memory while running, PcGb = memory the PC should have. All Uses values are estimates (no maker publishes them). PC: BlueStacks 8 (maker's recommended; minimum 4, bluestacks.com system requirements), Android Studio 8 (developer.android.com/studio minimum), LDPlayer 4 (ldplayer.net, minimum), MuMu 4 (mumuplayer.com, minimum), MEmu 4 (estimate; maker lists 2 minimum). Detection: candidate paths, then Uninstall DisplayName start -> InstallLocation + relative path (`UninstallRegistry`); MuMu's bundled adb is `nx_main\adb.exe`, used when no SDK adb exists. Mock-up differences: header and row paddings come from `TextBlock.menutext`/`Button.menurow`, not the mock-up's grid; "Not installed" header carries column labels only when nothing is installed.

**All actions page (16 Nov 2026, mock-up `docs/mockups/sidebar-all-actions.html`).** `Pages/AllActions/`: a normal page, `NavGroup = NavGroups.Pinned` (new, no heading), shown in a second `NavList` (`PinnedNav`) at the sidebar's bottom under an `Border.hrule`; `ShellViewModel.PinnedPages` / `SelectedPinnedPage` mirror the footer's selection sync. Rows read the live `HotkeyRegistry.Actions`, grouped by `Section` and `PickerHeading` (the entries' own fields, the same the Choose-the-action window uses); Run = `HotkeyRegistry.Run(id)` (through the gate); "Show on its page" = `ShellViewModel.OpenPage(category)` (does nothing when no page has that title or the search hides it). Differences: Up/Down order is main list, All actions, then the footer's App settings button, then round to Displays (the footer stays in the chain). Unverified: layout at the narrowest window, light theme, the row's keyboard use (the row template is a plain `ListBox.rows compact` like Shortcuts), a long action title.

## Smart Devices redesign: Ready vs Setup, teach on and off, emulator menu v3, profiles, icons (16 Nov 2026) - compiled only (Release throwaway builds of the app and the test project, 0 warnings, 0 errors, folders deleted); tests NOT run (owner's instruction); adb, kasa and the emulators never driven; nothing seen on screen
Built to `docs/mockups/smart-devices-setup-vs-ready.html` (layout master), with the approved additions `method-menu-briefs.html`, `script-profiles.html` and `device-icons.html` (its header layout supersedes the master's where they differ). Code map: `ARCHITECTURE.md` (Smart Devices row and the shared-controls row). Supersedes the "set-up checklist", the closing switch Check and the "Show the emulator window" switch described in the Devices sections above.
- Ready vs Setup: `DeviceRecord.IsReady` (scripts: `Verified`; simulation: `SimVerified` and taught). Not ready, or Edit setup…, = Setup mode: the method's stages (`SetupStage`, one open at a time; the first not done opens, and a stage finished by its own action opens the next). Cancel restores the copy taken at Edit setup (`DeviceRecord.Clone`/`CopyFrom`); for a device that was never ready Cancel is Delete (asks first). Finish setup is enabled when every stage is done. Delete is the cross in the header while in Setup.
- Hidden runs: `DeviceRuntime.InSetup` is the only thing that starts the emulator with its window (plus Show / Show emulator). `EmulatorKeepSettings.ShowWindow` and its menu row were removed (an old value in devices.json is ignored).
- State reading (Core, `Engine/Adb/State*.cs`, limits `CoreTiming.State*`): each "It's on now" / "It's off now" takes `Timing.DeviceSimTeachLooks` (3) looks `DeviceSimTeachGap` apart: the dump (texts, words, "has percent", the kinds of views in the card, checked / selected / enabled / activated of every element in the card) and `screencap -p` (decoded by `Png.Decode`; dominant colour and coloured share of the switch, mean colour of the card, 64-bit shape hash; colours on the hue-saturation disc, not RGB). Facts that are the same in every look of one state and differ between states become signals (weight 1); measures well apart (`StateMin*Separation`) with little spread become signals weighted by separation. A reading averages the signals per family (attributes, words, views, colour, shape) and the families by `StateWeight*`; Unknown below `StateReadThreshold`. Derive refuses ("on and off look the same") when nothing separates them or a taught look would be misread. The switch is found again by `StateTarget` (a card text present in every look, the switch's offset from it, id and class; its words only when they did not change), so it survives scrolling and never takes a look-alike card's switch; the run scrolls up then down to find it. Recent confidence is smoothed; below `StateDriftWarn` the Ready view says so and offers Re-teach.
- Runs: steps up to the last Tap (the switch, `SimRunner.SwitchIndex`), read, tap only when needed (Toggle always), read again until the state changed; success only then; unknown = no tap (On/Off) and one plain sentence. Steps after the switch run after a confirmed change. An old recording's trailing Check with no words (the old switch check) is dropped on load (`DropOldStateCheck`); a new recording clears the teaching.
- Scripts profiles: `ScriptProfiles.BuiltIn` (Auto-detect, Tapo plug, Tapo bulb (both `--type smart`, encryption left to kasa), Kasa plug (`--type plug`)), version 1 each; devices store only the id, so they follow app updates. Own profiles in `DeviceSettings.Profiles` (backed up with devices.json), never touched by updates; the Advanced pane edits an own profile live and a built-in one only as a draft (Test runs the draft; Save as my profile… creates "My <name>" at once and its Name field renames it: the app has no text-input dialog). `{password}` never reaches the arguments: the word and the option before it are dropped and kasa reads `KASA_PASSWORD`. Toggle and the test read the State command when it answers "state: on/off"; a scripted device's tag says "set" unless read back.
- kasa lookup: the py launcher / Store alias is followed to python.exe (`sys.executable`), Python is asked for its scripts folders (`sysconfig`, main and user scheme), plus `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Python\pythoncore-*\Scripts` and `%APPDATA%\Python\*\Scripts`; `python -m kasa.cli` only after `--help` works (`python -m kasa` never works). The resolved path is logged at each look. Failures show kasa's own error line (`KasaCommand.ProblemLine`) in red with Copy details (masked log lines).
- App label: an app opened from the home screen takes the words under its icon; else `application-label`/`nonLocalizedLabel`; else the package's second part ("Tplink").
- Fixed on the way: `MenuRowList` bound its Title to `ElementName=Root` (a StackPanel without that property), so menu titles never showed; it now binds to the control. The three "Copy details" handlers are one helper (`Controls/CopyDetails`).
- Narrowest window (680 px, reasoning only): header = icon | name (2/5) | chip and tags (3/5, wrapping in place); every field row is a Grid with a star column (fields stretch, `MinWidth=0`), tags and pills in Auto columns or WrapPanels; Sign-in puts the password and its tag on a second line; the step rows wrap their sentence and put the note on its own line; the three menus are listmenus (names wrap, never trimmed). Both themes: only theme brushes, Palette colours and the device colour (an opaque glyph on a 25 % wash) are used.
- Differences from the mock-ups (as far as the masters allow):
  1. Header: the name is a field (device-icons) and shares the line with the chip and tags by 2/5 : 3/5; at the narrowest width the tags wrap under the chip inside that column.
  2. On / Off / Toggle sit on the second line with Show emulator and Edit setup… at its right (device-icons), not on the first line (master).
  3. A foot status line (last command; red when it failed, Copy details) shows in both modes; the master has none. The drift sentence with Re-teach is an approved addition.
  4. Delete is a cross in the header while in Setup (no mock-up shows delete).
  5. The list is `ListBox.nav.compact` (selected = accent wash and edge, the nav master) 140-190 px wide in `Border.pane.edged` with a `Border.sidebarpanel` divider; the mock-up's outline-only selection is not a master look.
  6. State tag: "unknown · read HH:mm" (caution) when a run could not read it; scripted devices say "set" unless the State command read it back.
  7. Stage headers are `Button.groupHeader` (hover wash, accent focus) inside `Border.stage` (4,2 inner space), so folded stages sit about 10 px in; the open stage's outline is 1 px accent, radius 8.
  8. Finish setup is `Button.pill.cardaction.main` (accent edge and words with the soft accent wash); the mock-up shows no wash.
  9. Scripts has six stages (Python, python-kasa, Fixed address, Sign-in, Profile, Test). The simulation's Test stage shows On / Off / Toggle pills with ticks and a note line; the teach stage shows one line after teaching ("Learned: … by colour") or the problem.
  10. Steps stage: "Test all" removed (the Test stage replaces it); Start recording sits on the stage's first line with the help sentence. Stage 1 lost the second "connected" tag beside the address (the state tag carries it).
  11. Emulator and Method menus: `FlyoutPresenter.listmenu` (260-300 px) instead of 330/340 px; "Get it ↗" is `Button.link` (underlined); the menu's notes were reworded to the mock-up's ("Official, no ads. Needs a virtual device." ...).
  12. Profiles: versions start at v1 (the mock-up's v3 is an example); the Tapo profiles use `--type smart` (coordinator's later instruction) rather than the mock-up's plain `kasa --host {ip} on`; own profiles get a Name field in the pane.
  13. Icons: 12 drawn glyphs (bulb, plug, lamp, fan, TV, computer, coffee maker, thermometer, speaker, power strip, heater, wall switch), 8 per row; colour dots are the shared `Swatches` (the chosen one larger with a ring).
  14. Test stage "Did it switch? Yes / No" shows only for scripts when nothing read the change back; simulation tests always read it back, so they never ask (a failed reading shows the re-teach sentence instead).
- Unverified: all of it on screen (layouts, both themes, the narrowest window, pop-downs, the eye, the icon pop-down), every adb call (`screencap -p` over exec-out on each emulator, uiautomator attributes such as `activated` that some Android versions do not write), the thresholds and weights on real apps (Tapo, Kasa, others), kasa's `--type smart` and `state` output on the installed version, python-kasa's env-var sign-in, the Python lookup on the owner's PC. The new tests (`StateDetectionTests`, the updated `SimulationTests` and `DevicesTests`) compile but were not run.
- Open: a device's own icon picture (`%APPDATA%\OmniControl\device-icons`) is not in backups; Cancel does not undo a password saved to Windows or a picture picked during the set-up; attribute facts are keyed by the element's kind and its order in the card, so an app that inserts an element of the same kind above the switch makes those facts disagree (other families still vote); Devices are still not in global search or Restore defaults.

## Scripts templates (16 Nov 2026) - compiled only (app and tests, throwaway builds); tests not run; nothing seen on screen
Built to `docs/mockups/script-templates.html`, which supersedes "Profile" in `script-profiles.html`. A template is a `ScriptProfile` (the code name stays; on screen it is "template"): On/Off/State, `NeedsSignIn`, `SignInNote`, `Stages` (`TemplateStage`), `Tips` (`ErrorTip`), `Brief`, `MenuTag`. Built-ins: Tapo, Kasa, Auto-detect; old ids tapo-plug, tapo-bulb, kasa-plug map in `ScriptProfiles.Of`. The set-up stages are made by `DeviceItem.MakeScriptStages` and rebuilt when the template changes; a confirmed template stage is kept per device in `DeviceRecord.StagesDone`. `KasaCommand.ProblemLine` drops noise lines and prefers the first "Raised error:" line; `DeviceService.RunTemplateAsync` swaps in a template tip. Differences from the mock-up: the chip has no accent edge while set-up is open; the menu shows "built-in" tags (and "slower" on Auto-detect) plus "mine"; "My templates" is a header, not only a rule; "Manage templates…" opens the Advanced pane and is left out during set-up (the pane lives in the Ready view); the Template row in the Ready view shows the name as text (the chip is in the header). Open: Auto-detect asks for a sign-in like Tapo, so an old Kasa on Auto-detect cannot finish set-up (use Kasa); Tapo bulbs lose the bulb default icon (pick it in the icon pop-down); no way yet to edit a template's stages or tips in the app (own templates copy them).

## Smart Devices: one device, several methods; list menu and multi-select (16 Nov 2026) - compiled only (app and tests, Release); tests not run; nothing seen on screen; adb and kasa never driven
Built to `docs/mockups/device-multiple-methods.html` (option B) plus the owner's additions (list menu, multi-select, Ready-view delete, clipping). Code map: `ARCHITECTURE.md` (Smart Devices row). Supersedes the "one method per device" wording of the Devices sections above: the Method chip now belongs to the method being set up, and the Template row with Advanced… moved from the Ready view into a scripts method's set-up (under its stages).
- Data: `DeviceRecord` = name, icon, auto-check and the ordered `Methods` (`MethodRecord`: kind and its own set-up). An old record is migrated at load (`DeviceRecord.Migrate`, via `[JsonExtensionData]`): its fields become one method whose id is the device's id, so the password (Credential Manager `OmniControl/device/<method id>`) and the old shortcuts still fit. Merging an old backup into a newer file drops the old flat fields of a device that already has methods. Backup and merge cover everything (methods are a list with ids inside devices.json).
- Runs (`DeviceService.Run.cs`): the ready methods in order; a method that fails (missing tool, unreachable, sign-in, emulator, unsure reading) falls through to the next. A Toggle that fell through becomes an exact On or Off (the opposite of the last known state, unknown = off), so a method that may have switched before failing is never undone. Status: "Last command 08:02 · Simulation: turned on in 3.1 s · Scripts didn't work" (the method prefix only when the device has more than one); all methods failing gives one line with each method's reason, and Copy details joins their log lines. The simulation runs now also say "Last command" (they said "Last run"). Check reads by the same order.
- Actions: "<name>: On / Off / Toggle" keep their ids (`devices.<device id>.<power>`); "<name>: On via Scripts" per method, id `devices.method.<method id>.<power>`, so they follow a merged method. Two methods of one kind are named "Scripts 1", "Scripts 2" (`DeviceRecord.NameOf`); the titles are made again on reorder.
- Merge into… (list right-click, `ListMenuFlyout` + `MenuRowList`; opens a second menu with the target devices): asks, then the chosen devices' methods go after the target's, set-ups and passwords kept; the merged devices' order-action shortcuts move to the "via" actions of their first method; their own "via" shortcuts stay (same ids). Mouse buttons, gestures and rules that used a merged device's order actions are NOT moved (they point at an id that is gone): open item.
- List: `SelectionMode="Multiple"` (Ctrl, Shift, Ctrl+A); right-click Copy, Cut, Paste (below), Duplicate, Merge into…, Delete; Ctrl+C/X/V/D and Delete do the same. Delete asks once (`Confirm.Delete`), clears the actions' shortcuts and unregisters them. Copy/Duplicate makes a full copy with new device and method ids and no password (the status line says to type the sign-in again when a template needs one); a copied scripts method stays ready, so its switch is off with "Not ready: the account's password is not saved" until the password is saved. Cut only marks; Paste moves (reorder only).
- Setup: a method row click, Enter or its "Edit setup…" opens that method's stages; "+ add method" (dashed, opens the Method list) adds one at the end and opens its set-up, Cancel removes it again. The header cross: Ready view = delete the device; Setup = remove that method (asks), or delete the device when it is the only one. The drift sentence names the method when there are several; Re-teach opens that method.
- Clipping: the header is now icon | name | cross on one line and a full-width wrapping line under it (Method / Template chips in a set-up; ready, state, Check now, Auto-check in the Ready view), instead of a 3/5 right column. `Controls/PasswordField` (shared, only user here) puts its tag on its own line under the box so the box and Save keep their room; the saved-password placeholder is "Saved · type to replace". On / Off / Toggle / Show emulator share one WrapPanel.
- Mouse-first choices (rule 5): reordering methods is drag only (no keyboard move); the Merge into… target list is a menu.
- Differences from the mock-up: the method rows put the "ready"/"set-up" tag and an "Edit setup…" link beside the title (wrapping together) and the speed/where note at the right in small muted words; the note of a method after the first reads "fallback · <where>"; the Simulation's speed word is "slower" (no measured figure); the "Actions: …" line under the list is not shown (the actions are in Shortcuts and the action picker); the title "Methods (drag to order; first that works is used)" is the mock-up's wording.
- Unverified: everything on screen (narrowest window, both themes, the two-step Merge menu reopening at the pointer, Tapped on a row not firing after a drag); fall-through against real devices; tests (new `DevicesTests` for migration, naming, readiness, copy) compile but were not run. The build was checked in a copy of the tree because the screenshot job's in-progress Core files (`Mp4Writer.cs`, `GifWriter.cs`) did not compile at the time.
- Open: mouse/gesture/rule uses of a merged device's actions are not moved; a merged-away device in an old backup comes back on merge as its own device sharing a method id (its password is then kept while either holds it); no keyboard reorder of methods.

- Tray panel edit mode (open item): the separate edit lists (drag by grip, "Not available now" rows for missing ids) were replaced by crosses and the right-click menu on the real tiles; elements whose ids match nothing are no longer listed, so they cannot be removed from the panel until they are back.

- Repeated clicking (17 Nov 2026, spec `docs/mockups/repeated-clicking.html`): one master action `mouse.repeatclick`; its settings are per input (`ActionSettings.Clicking`, `MouseOptions.RepeatClicking`), a hotkey or rule runs it as a toggle with defaults. "While held" works on mouse buttons only (`HotkeyAction.RunHeld`): `MouseService` swallows the physical press and release (unless "also send the button's normal click" is on), so it can be mapped on the very button it clicks (e.g. Left); our clicks are marked by `InputSender` so the hook never re-triggers the mapping; the release ends it and a held button ("Hold down") is always let go. Differences from the mock-up: Speed is a number card, not a slider; the badge is the OSD text pop-up (element "Repeated clicking", refreshed each second) with the Play icon, as there is no click icon. "Where" (18 Nov): `MouseOptions.ClickWhere` At the pointer | At a fixed point, with `ClickPoint` (`ActionOption.Point`, `OptionKind.Point`, stored "x,y" physical pixels by `Services/ScreenPointText`, shown by `Controls/PointField`, picked with the ONE `Controls/PointPicker.PickAsync()`; overlay `Controls/PointPickerOverlay`, mouse first: click picks, Esc or right click cancels, Enter on the row opens it). Choice made: the pointer is moved to the point when it starts and put back there before a click if it was nudged (unless "Stop when the mouse moves" is on, which then stops it), and it is NOT returned to where it was afterwards; "At a fixed point" with no point picked yet clicks at the pointer. The overlay is a plain dim (no frozen screenshot) and has no keyboard picking (mouse design, overrule if wanted). `HotkeyRegistry.RunHeld` now passes the one gate (`Permitted`, asked on the UI thread with at most `Timing.GateAskWait`; a refusal or no answer returns a do-nothing stopper so the button is not run once instead), is guarded like `RunNow` and raises `Ran`. Open: an input without a release (wheel, gesture) runs it as a toggle; the Actions and hotkeys window shows a key-bound action without options. Unverified: all behaviour on screen (the owner tests).

## One action window and action defaults (17 Nov 2026) - mock-up `docs/mockups/unified-action-window.html`; Release build only, nothing run or seen

- **One window.** `PickerWindow` + `PickerModel` are the only action window; manage mode (menu) and pick mode (a button, gesture or step, `PickerModel.Target`) differ only in the top bar (manage only), the settings' first row and the foot. `ActionsWindowService` stays as the owner of the top bar's data (shortcut sets, Clear all, Restore defaults, search entries); there was no second window class left to delete.
- **Defaults live with the keys.** `StoredHotkey.Options` (non-default values only) in `hotkeys.json`, so they travel with the shortcut set and "Restore defaults" puts them back. A decision: per shortcut set, because that is where the keys live. `HotkeyRegistry.Settings` (`IActionSettings`, set in `ShellViewModel`) decides which settings an action has (`MouseActionCatalog.OwnOptions`) and what values mean (`MouseOptions.RunOf`).
- **Who uses them.** A hotkey press, "Run now" and a rule's Run step run the action with its defaults once it has any (else exactly as before). A mouse button, gesture or gesture-rule step that has none of the action's own settings stored and no `own=1` mark resolves to the defaults in `HotkeyRegistry.Run(id, settings)` / `RunHeld`. A binding that already had one of the action's own settings stored keeps them as its own copy (migration without rewriting any file); turning the switch off writes `own=1` so a copy equal to the declared defaults stays a copy.
- **Only the action's own settings follow the defaults** (program, arguments, folder, step, countdown, force-close, repeated clicking). What only an input has (when it runs, times, repeat, "also send", keep the original click) stays on the input, and is not shown for a hotkey (a key has no such thing, so the menu's settings leave them out).
- **Open items.** Tray and Automation step pickers list actions without settings, so they show no switch (their runs use the defaults). The top bar is not shown in pick mode (the mock-up says it "may"). The Automation rule editors and the tray panel's picker still say "Use this action" (no target name given there); the mouse and gesture cards say "Use for <card>". A custom action's editor (`HotkeyAction.Editor`) keeps writing its own values and has no defaults row.

## Windows' screenshot keys switch (17 Nov 2026, docs/mockups/windows-snipping-switch.html)
- Built: toggle option `ScreenshotFields.WindowsKeys` directly under "Same settings for every screenshot action" in every screenshot action's settings (a global value, kept in `ScreenshotSettings.TurnOffWindowsKeys`, never in an action's own set), the action "Turn off Windows' screenshot keys: toggle" (`screenshot.windowskeys`, section Screenshot), owner `Screenshot/WindowsKeysSwitch`, registry in Core `Engine/SnippingKeys.cs`. On remembers the old values (`WindowsKeysBackup`) and sets `HKCU\Control Panel\Keyboard\PrintScreenKeyForSnippingEnabled`=0 and adds "S" to `HKCU\...\Explorer\Advanced\DisabledHotkeys`; off puts them back (only the S OmniControl added is removed). Start-up re-applies it when on. The Actions and hotkeys window's "Restore defaults" turns it off too (a second `IRestorable` in `ShellViewModel.Restore.cs`).
- Differs from the mock-up: the switch row has no note line and no "applies after sign-out" tag (the shared switch row cannot show either without changing `PickerMenu.axaml`); the note is part of the label and the sign-out fact is said once on the status line when it is turned on or off.
- Open: if `screenshots.json` is wiped or OmniControl is uninstalled while it is on, the remembered values are lost and Windows stays as changed (turn it off first). Not tested on a real Windows (rule 7): that Print Screen follows at once without a broadcast, and that Explorer picks up DisabledHotkeys after sign-out.

## Microphone mute key, fast mute tiles, one tile mark (17 Nov 2026, 0.1.6)
- Built: the Toggle microphone mute action has a live setting "Mute the way your keyboard does" (a plain switch row with a note; `VolumePageViewModel.MicKey.cs`, shared `ActionEditor` as the screenshot actions use). Turning it on with no key learned listens for `Timing.MicKeyLearn` (a low-level keyboard hook, `Core/Engine/Keyboard/KeyLearner`; the key is not swallowed; keys in the first `MicKeyLearnSettle` and OmniControl's own are ignored), stores vk, scan code and extended flag (`mic-mute-key.json`) and says "Microphone mute key learned." Nothing arriving: the status line says "This keyboard's mute key can't be copied; the normal mute is used." and the switch is stored off. The mute (hotkey, tray tile, rule) then sends that key through `InputSender.Tap` (down and up in one call, so never left pressed; at most `MicKeyBurstMax` per `MicKeyBurstWindow`), shows the OSD at once, and `MicKeyCheckDelay` later reads the real mute: as wanted = nothing more; not = Windows' own mute is applied and one line goes to the errors log (once per run).
- The check keeps the microphone state's liveness `Reading` while it waits, so the tray tile's grey dot comes from `StateEntry.Liveness` like a smart device's. That was already the only path (`TrayTileItem.Read` reads `StateEntry.Liveness`; no tile, page or state sets the mark by hand; grepped in `Tray/`, the flyout view and the pages); the microphone state is the new user.
- Fast tray: `OsdService` raises `VolumeWatched` / `MicrophoneWatched` from its existing Windows change watchers (no extra COM watcher); the Volume page syncs the master or default microphone row (which notifies `StateRegistry`) at once, keeping a level it just set itself for `SliderEchoGrace`.
- Differences and limits: the app command (WM_APPCOMMAND, microphone mute) is neither learned nor sent: a low-level keyboard hook cannot see it and learning it needs a hidden shell-hook window; only a key that arrives as a key is copied. The learn status ("Press your microphone mute key now") shows as the row's tag when the switch is turned on; the switch stays visually on after a failed learn until the pane is shown again (the pane has no refresh call). A key sent with SendInput is marked injected, and some makers' software ignores injected keys: then the check falls back to the normal mute. The mouse, gesture and rule pickers show the same setting (the editor is global).
- Unverified (rule 7, nothing launched or pressed): the hook against a real mute key, the maker's software reacting to the sent key, the tile's dot timing, the pane's look. Compiled into a throwaway folder (0 errors).

## Emulator chip on the Simulation row (17 Nov 2026, `docs/mockups/emulator-chip.html`)

- The chip (`Button.selector.running` while up, phone glyph `device-mobile`) sits in `Pages/Devices/MethodList.axaml` between the title and the note, simulation rows only. Click = `SimulationViewModel.ChipCommand` (start hidden through `EmulatorKeeper.StartAsync`, or `CloseNowAsync`); right-click (`ContextRequested`, `ListMenuFlyout` at the pointer) = `Pages/Devices/KeepRunningMenu` with `EmulatorActions` (Close now or Start, Show) shown at its top. The same `KeepRunningMenu` is setup step 1's Keep running menu (without those rows): one view, one model (`KeepOptions`, `KeepSwitches`, `IdleMinutes`).
- Memory: `SimulationSetup` reads `Emulators.MemoryUsed` (working set, Core) every `Timing.EmulatorMemoryRefresh` on a background timer, caches it (`MemoryOf`) and raises `Changed` only when the shown tenth of a GB moves. The old direct read in `StateText` is gone.
- State: `devices.emulator.running` (on/off, owner `DeviceService`, `DeviceService.States.cs`; set = start hidden or close the first simulation method's emulator), notified on `Simulation.Changed`; its `Liveness` is "acting" while `EmulatorKeeper.Busy` (starting, showing or closing).
- Differences from the mock-up: no separate checking badge on the chip itself (the shared badge shows on the state's tray tile); the chip shows the same text while starting or closing (only the state's acting mark changes); with several simulation devices the state follows the first one's emulator and any one being up counts as running. Not run or looked at by an agent (rule 7): the layout at the narrowest window, both themes and the right-click menu position are for the owner to check.

## Suggested keys switch (17 Nov 2026, 0.1.6; docs/mockups/suggested-keys-switch.html)
- Built: `HotkeyAction.SuggestedKeySet` (with the existing `SuggestedKey`) on the four rotation actions; `HotkeyRegistry.SuggestedKeys.cs` holds the rules; `Hotkeys/SuggestedKeysSwitch` + `SuggestedKeysView` is the shared control, shown on the Orientation card through `GalleryItem.Extra` (third column of `GalleryRow`, drawn by the data template in `App.axaml`).
- State and the user's own keys are one meta entry `suggested-keys:displays.rotate` in the saved bindings (hotkeys.json and each shortcut set), `Options` = `on` and `own:<action id>`. Fresh install (nothing saved at start) with no keys on the members: on. Anything else without an entry: off. After any import (a set switched, Restore defaults) a set marked on whose members no longer hold their keys is turned off (so Restore defaults leaves it off with no keys).
- Differences from the mock-up: the mock-up is a one-line sketch, so sizes are the shared styles (`KeycapRow.small`, `muted small`, `TagView`); keycaps show "Ctrl", "Alt" and one cap "arrows"; the switch is reachable by Tab and mouse but not by the card's Up/Down/Left/Right keys (open item: keyboard step into the Extra); the suggested Left/Right now follow the mock-up (Left Portrait, Right Portrait flipped), the old Right Portrait / Left Portrait flipped suggestion is gone (bound keys of existing users are untouched). The orientation actions have no `Family`, so the pickers still list them as four rows.
- Restoring old keys skips (and says so in the status line) a key another action took meanwhile.
- Unverified: not run or seen. Only compiled: the one build error left is `Fx/FxDeviceSource.cs` (`VirtualCables`, the other agent's work). Layout at the narrowest width with the caution tag, both themes, and keyboard use are unchecked.

## Hotkeys master switch and Restore defaults (17 Nov 2026, 0.1.6; docs/mockups/hotkeys-master-switch.html)
- Built: `Hotkeys/HotkeysSwitch` (app setting `AppSettings.HotkeysOn`, default on; state `app.hotkeys.on` "Hotkeys on"; action `app.hotkeys.toggle` "Hotkeys: on/off", NeverBlock, filed under App settings via `AppSettingsPageViewModel.Name`). `HotkeyRegistry.PauseKeys` makes `Rebind` register only NeverBlock keys while paused (the same way `Hold` does), so the keys stay saved. The window's top bar shows the switch (`ActionsWindowService.HotkeysSwitch`, `PickerWindow.axaml`). Mouse buttons, gestures and rules are not paused, as the mock-up says.
- Restore defaults fix: `HotkeyRegistry.DefaultBindings()` (used by the Actions window's restore and its "at defaults" check) gives each suggested-keys member its suggested key unless taken; `ImportAll` then calls `AdoptSuggestedKeys`, which turns a set on when its members hold exactly its suggested keys (also after an undo to such a state). The meta's remembered own keys are empty then, as on a fresh install.
- Differences from the mock-up (a one-line sketch): switch sits in the bar's left group right after the window name, not in the empty middle; tooltip added; the switch is not a preset-bound value (not per shortcut set).
- Unverified: only compiled (the remaining build error is `Fx/FxDeviceSource.cs`, another agent's). Not run: pausing, un-pausing via the action's key, state toggling from the tray, narrow-window layout, both themes, Restore defaults on a changed set.

## OSD custom position and screens (17 Nov 2026, 0.1.6; docs/mockups/osd-custom-position.html)
- Built, all 5 parts. (1) The OSD page's Position is a `MenuChoiceItem` card (the shared chip + `MenuRowList` menu): the 7 built-in places, a thin line, muted "Your places" + the named ones (ticked = in use), a thin line, accent "Custom position...", muted "Manage places...". `MenuRowItem` got `KeepsOpen`, `IsAccent`, `IsMuted` (styles `Button.menurow.accent/.muted`), `ChipMenu` got `ChipTag` and `ChipAlignment` (a card's chip is a keyboard stop, Tag "scope"). Named places are `OsdSettings.Places` (osd.json), shown to the preset manager by `OsdPlacePresets` (group `osd.places`, section OSD, so each place also gets a "Use OSD place" shortcut action automatically). (2) `Pages/Osd/OsdPlacementWindow` (cards Screen, Anchor, Across, Up/Down, Name; foot OK / Cancel). (3) `Osd/OsdPreview`: the live OSD with a dashed accent edge (`Rectangle.osdframe`); drag (snaps to edges, margin lines, centre lines within `Timing.OsdSnap`), arrow keys 1 px / Shift 10 px after a click on it; sliders and fields follow. (4) "Show on" card beside Position: All screens (default, fresh install and Restore defaults), Screen with the mouse, then a ticked list of the screens (several can be ticked; the last untick goes back to all screens); the place is applied on every chosen screen with one `OsdWindow` per screen. (5) z-order: see below.
- Offsets are in device-independent pixels from the anchor (`OsdSpot`): Across is inward from a left or right anchor and to the right from a middle one, Up/Down is down from a top anchor and up from a bottom or middle one; the labels follow the anchor. The pop-up is kept inside the screen's working area, so an offset can never push it off.
- **Mouse-first choices (rule 5, owner can overrule):** dragging the preview is mouse first (the keyboard nudges it only once it was clicked and has the keyboard); the Position and Show on menus are mouse-first pop-downs (the chip is keyboard reachable, Enter opens it); there is no wheel on those chips (a menu with a "Custom position..." row has no sensible next value).
- **Z-order fix, cause not seen:** I could not run the app, so the cause of the switch showing through the open drop-down is not confirmed. Two shared rules in `Theme.axaml`: the card holding the keyboard (`ListBox.rows > ListBoxItem:focus-within`) is drawn above its neighbours (`ZIndex` 1), and a ComboBox's list (`Border#PopupBorder`) gets the opaque panel colour `BrushSidebar`. The Position drop-down itself is gone (it is now a menu), so check any other drop-down over a switch.
- **Differences from the mock-up:** (a) OK is the window foot's confirm button (`Button.usebtn`) beside `Button.primary.close` Cancel, as in the Combine devices window, not a `Button.primary`; (b) Across has a direction word like the Up/Down one ("Right from left", "Left from right", "Right from center") because its sign differs per anchor; (c) a slider shows "+96 px" for a positive value (the shared `SliderItem` text); (d) the preview is its own window (`OsdPreview`), not the real pop-up window (that one ignores the mouse and cannot be dragged), drawn with the same `OsdView` and the same `OsdPlacement` maths; (e) picking another anchor keeps the OSD where it is and recomputes the offsets (the mock-up does not say); (f) a name that is already a place replaces that place on OK; (g) screens are called "Screen 2 · 2560 x 1440 (primary)" in Windows' order, not by monitor model; a screen is remembered by Windows' name for it (`Screen.DisplayName`), else by its position.
- Values: `Timing.OsdMargin/OsdSnap/OsdNudge/OsdNudgeLarge/OsdOffsetMax/OsdRoundingSlack` (DIP distances, no new time value was needed); sizes `OsdFrame*`, `AnchorCell*`, `AnchorFrame*`, `OsdPlacementWidth` in `App.axaml`.
- Unverified (compiled only; the build's one remaining error is `Fx/FxDeviceSource.cs`, another agent's): everything on screen. Not seen: the Position and Show on menus and their look in both themes and at the narrowest window, the anchor picker's keys and focus frame, the placement window, dragging, snapping and nudging, the dashed edge, a second screen (one window per screen, the screen with the mouse, `Screen.DisplayName` as a key), Restore defaults, the preset manager's places card, the z-order fix. The new `OsdPlacement` maths has no unit test yet.

## Display feeds and the Parametric spectrum (17 Nov 2026, unverified on a real PC)

- A VST2 plug-in or equaliser the master runs in Equalizer APO is fed for display only (`DisplayFeeds`): loopback of the master's APO device, processed by the editor copy (`PluginStage`) and discarded; runs while its editor window is open (not its settings list); stops on close, on a method change (`EffectsEngine.StatusChanged`), and before shut-down unloads plug-ins. The FX window's tag "Display fed with what you hear" (`EffectsRules.DisplayFed*`) shows for the selected effect while any feed or heard spectrum runs for it.
- The spectrum starts when the `EqGraph` attaches to the visual tree and ends when it detaches (`EqPanelItem.WatchSpectrum`). Own engine: the `EqStage` tap (input of the equaliser, the real signal). APO: the same loopback.
- Skipped on purpose: a program on a cable with Equalizer APO (`ApoCable`) is not fed (the device to hear is not the device the program plays on alone: it would include other programs); a device folder bound to another device than the playing one is not told apart (the master's playing device is heard); VST3 plug-ins (not run by Equalizer APO) get no feed.
- Band circles are half size (`EqBandCircle` 11); numbers are not drawn in them any more (the 10-band "crowded" mode went with it). The grab radius (`HitRadius` 16) is unchanged.
- Open: the plug-in's `Process` runs on the tap thread while the UI thread may call the same editor copy (as the engine already does); a plug-in that is not thread-safe could misbehave. Never run on a real PC here; the owner tests.

Now `States/CommonGroups.All` (`CommonGroup`: name, icon, column) is the one place for order and column (left: Screens, Sound; right: Power, Smart devices; Now playing was folded into Sound 18 Nov), `Pages/FixedColumnsPanel` (columns at least `FixedColumnMinWidth` 250, gap `FixedColumnGap`) reads the column from each block (`IColumnPlaced`) and goes to one column in list order when narrow. `Rebuild` always makes all four groups; one with nothing says "Not available" (no master switch). Offline: the existing one-sentence note; it no longer says "Not connected" while the link is up. Loading is only waiting for the link's message (nothing blocks the UI thread). Open: a PC that is offline and never probed shows its note, not a spinner.
- **Slider fit.** `Controls/LabelFitPanel` (half cards) and `LeadFitPanel` now keeps `MainMinWidth` even when the card is narrower (it may then overflow the card edge rather than hide). Half cards use value box minimum 48 (was 32). Plain (full-width) slider cards were not changed; a companion line is unchanged. `LabelMinWidth` 56 is typed in `SliderRow.axaml`. Narrow window, both themes, search results unchecked.
- **No-clip audit (18 Nov).** Shared fixes: `TextBlock.cardname` / `c|IconText.cardname` (wraps, `MaxWidth` = `CardLabelMaxWidth`) on every full-width card name (Toggle, Choice, MenuChoice, Gallery, Button, File, Text, Anchor, Scope, Slider, EQ band, graphic band, plug-in param rows); `IconText` always wraps; `ComboBox.choice`, `c|ChipMenu.choice` and the gallery now sit in the column right of the name (stretch, centred, at most `ChoiceMaxWidth` / `GalleryMaxWidth`) instead of a fixed 220/300 spanning the label column, so they shrink and never cover it (search results keep their right-aligned 220 `Width`); resources `CardLabelColumnMinWidth` 110, `CardLabelMinWidth` 56, `SliderBarMinWidth` 60, `SliderValueMinWidth` 48 (now the default of `SliderField`, typed `ValueMinWidth="48"` removed), `SliderValueGap` 8; the companion-switch slider column and the Button card's description column have minimums. Visual change: a drop-down or gallery is centred in the room right of the name, not over the whole card. Open: `SliderFieldMinWidth` 116 is still the typed sum of the three slider resources (XAML cannot add); Button card pills cannot wrap; gallery previews (colour dots) may clip below about 200 px of gallery width; `MemberLevelView` value now has the 48 minimum. ## Smart device state on the OSD (18 Nov 2026), built to `docs/mockups/smart-device-osd.html`; compiled only (Release build into %TEMP%), nothing run (rule 7)
- `OsdMessage` gained `LibraryIcon` (any icon value, drawn by `Controls/IconGlyph` through `IconResources`) and `OsdTone` (`On` accent, `Off` muted, `Failed` red; `Normal` = every older message, unchanged). `OsdService.SmartDevicesElement` ("Smart devices", text element, so its switch is on by default and appears on the OSD page by itself) and `ShowDeviceNote` (any thread). `DeviceService` takes the `Action<OsdMessage>` in its constructor and says: a successful On/Off/Toggle (the state it left), a read that finds a different state than the last known one (a first read or an unchanged one is silent), a failed command ("Couldn't turn on / off / switch", warning icon `IconLibrary.Warning`, red; a user's Stop is not a failure).
- Differences from the mock-up: a failure shows the warning icon, not the device's own; the icon is `DeviceService.IconOf` (the one the tile, state and actions show); a picture picked before the icon library (`IconImage`) is not used by the tile either, so the OSD shows the library icon. Unverified on screen: both themes, OSD styles, a user (`user:`) icon, the first pop-up's speed.

## Power actions held back after start-up (18 Nov 2026, 0.1.8); compiled and relaunched only, no power action was run (rule 7)
- **Grace only for trigger-started rules.** `Automation/PowerGuard` now holds back sleep, hibernate, shut down, restart and sign out only when the run's chain was started by a trigger (`RunChain.ByPerson` false), within `Timing.PowerActionGrace` (2 min) of OmniControl starting OR of a wake the power journal confirmed (`PowerGuard.Woke`, called from `AutomationService.OnWoke`, which still queues the wake trigger exactly as before). A key, mouse button, gesture, Run now, the tray, a rule started by its own hotkey (`RunByAction` with no chain) and a person on another PC (`OtherPcsService.Refusal` with no incoming chain) are never held. Before, the grace applied to everything and was measured on `DateTime.UtcNow`, so a clock set back (time sync after a wake) could hold every power action for as long as the clock had jumped; it is now `Environment.TickCount64`.
- **Status words.** "Sleep was not done: a rule can't do that within 2 minutes after OmniControl started / the PC woke."; a second request while a countdown shows now says so (it was silent); a failure inside the countdown or the call is logged to `errors-log.txt` and said (it was an unobserved task).
- **Core.** `PowerCommands.Sleep` / `Hibernate` switch on SeShutdownPrivilege in the process's own token first (SetSuspendState can refuse without it) and run on a worker (they return only after the wake, and froze the UI till then). Shut down, restart and sign out now read shutdown.exe's exit code (`CoreTiming.ShutdownExeWaitMs`), so a refusal is reported instead of silence.
- **Open items.** (1) Two OmniControl processes were running at once: fixed by `Services/SingleInstance` (see "Taskbar breaking while OmniControl runs"). (2) A rule on another PC that the person started there arrives with that PC's rule chain, so it counts as trigger-started here (the protocol carries rule ids only). (3) A rule whose power step was held still shows "Ran" (the reason is in the status line only). (4) `Services/Grace.Within` uses `DateTime.UtcNow`; a clock set back makes every echo grace last too long (not changed here).

The offline sentence says asleep, shut down or off from the PC's own notice (the mock-up says only "asleep"). The green tag now carries the tick ("Can wake from sleep" with a tick glyph in its words).
